   171→def _import_based_mapping(
   172→    graph: dict, test_files: set[str], production_files: set[str],
   173→) -> set[str]:
   174→    """Map test files to production files via import edges.
   175→
   176→    For test files in the graph, uses the graph's import edges.
   177→    For test files NOT in the graph (external), reads the file and parses
   178→    Python/TS import statements to find references to production files.
   179→    """
   180→    tested = set()
   181→    # Build module-name→path index for resolving test imports
   182→    prod_by_module: dict[str, str] = {}
   183→    root_str = str(PROJECT_ROOT) + os.sep
   184→    for pf in production_files:
   185→        # Convert to relative path from PROJECT_ROOT for module name mapping
   186→        rel_pf = pf[len(root_str):] if pf.startswith(root_str) else pf
   187→        mod = rel_pf.replace("/", ".").replace("\\", ".")
   188→        if mod.endswith(".py"):
   189→            mod = mod[:-3]
   190→        elif mod.endswith(".ts") or mod.endswith(".tsx"):
   191→            mod = mod.rsplit(".", 1)[0]
   192→        prod_by_module[mod] = pf
   193→        # __init__.py: also map the package path (e.g. "desloppify.lang" → __init__.py)
   194→        if mod.endswith(".__init__"):
   195→            prod_by_module[mod[:-len(".__init__")]] = pf
   196→        # Also map just the final module name: "utils" → path
   197→        parts = mod.split(".")
   198→        if parts:
   199→            prod_by_module[parts[-1]] = pf
   200→
   201→    for tf in test_files:
   202→        entry = graph.get(tf)
   203→        if entry is not None:
   204→            for imp in entry.get("imports", set()):
   205→                if imp in production_files:
   206→                    tested.add(imp)
   207→        else:
   208→            # External test file: parse imports from source
   209→            tested |= _parse_test_imports(tf, production_files, prod_by_module)
   210→
   211→    # Expand barrel files (index.ts/index.tsx) — one level deep
   212→    barrel_files = [
   213→        f for f in tested
   214→        if os.path.basename(f) in ("index.ts", "index.tsx")
   215→    ]
   216→    for bf in barrel_files:
   217→        tested |= _resolve_barrel_reexports(bf, production_files)
   218→
   219→    return tested
   220→
   221→
   222→_PY_IMPORT_RE = re.compile(
   223→    r"^\s*(?:from\s+([\w.]+)\s+import|import\s+([\w.]+))", re.MULTILINE
   224→)
   225→_TS_IMPORT_RE = re.compile(
   226→    r"""(?:from|import)\s+['"]([^'"]+)['"]""", re.MULTILINE
   227→)
   228→
   229→
   230→_TS_EXTENSIONS = ["", ".ts", ".tsx", "/index.ts", "/index.tsx"]
   231→
   232→
   233→def _resolve_ts_import(
   234→    spec: str, test_path: str, production_files: set[str],
   235→) -> str | None:
   236→    """Resolve a TS import specifier to a production file path.
   237→
   238→    Handles @/ and ~/ aliases (via SRC_PATH), relative paths (./  ../),
   239→    and probes common extensions (.ts, .tsx, /index.ts, /index.tsx).
   240→    """
   241→    if spec.startswith("@/") or spec.startswith("~/"):
   242→        base = Path(str(SRC_PATH) + "/" + spec[2:])
   243→    elif spec.startswith("."):
   244→        test_dir = Path(test_path).parent
   245→        base = (test_dir / spec).resolve()
   246→    else:
   247→        return None
   248→
   249→    for ext in _TS_EXTENSIONS:
   250→        candidate = str(Path(str(base) + ext))
   251→        if candidate in production_files:
   252→            return candidate
   253→        # Also try resolving symlinks / normalising for on-disk check
   254→        try:
   255→            resolved = str(Path(str(base) + ext).resolve())
   256→            if resolved in production_files:
   257→                return resolved
   258→        except OSError:
   259→            pass
   260→    return None
   261→
   262→
   263→_REEXPORT_RE = re.compile(
   264→    r"""^export\s+(?:\{[^}]*\}|\*)\s+from\s+['"]([^'"]+)['"]""", re.MULTILINE
   265→)
   266→
   267→
   268→def _resolve_barrel_reexports(
   269→    filepath: str, production_files: set[str],
   270→) -> set[str]:
   271→    """Resolve re-exports from a barrel file (index.ts/index.tsx).
   272→
   273→    One level deep only — no recursive barrel chaining.
   274→    Returns the set of production files re-exported by this barrel.
   275→    """
   276→    try:
   277→        content = Path(filepath).read_text()
   278→    except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError):
   279→        return set()
   280→
   281→    results = set()
   282→    for m in _REEXPORT_RE.finditer(content):
   283→        spec = m.group(1)
   284→        resolved = _resolve_ts_import(spec, filepath, production_files)
   285→        if resolved:
   286→            results.add(resolved)
   287→    return results
   288→
   289→
   290→def _parse_test_imports(
   291→    test_path: str, production_files: set[str], prod_by_module: dict[str, str],
   292→) -> set[str]:
   293→    """Parse import statements from a test file and resolve to production files."""
   294→    tested = set()
   295→    try:
   296→        content = Path(test_path).read_text()
   297→    except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError):
   298→        return tested
   299→
   300→    # Try Python imports
   301→    for m in _PY_IMPORT_RE.finditer(content):
   302→        module = m.group(1) or m.group(2)
   303→        if not module:
   304→            continue
   305→        # Try full module path and progressively shorter prefixes
   306→        parts = module.split(".")
   307→        for i in range(len(parts), 0, -1):
   308→            candidate = ".".join(parts[:i])
   309→            if candidate in prod_by_module:
   310→                tested.add(prod_by_module[candidate])
   311→                break
   312→
   313→    # Try TS imports
   314→    for m in _TS_IMPORT_RE.finditer(content):
   315→        spec = m.group(1)
   316→        if not spec:
   317→            continue
   318→        # First try proper resolution (relative paths, @/ alias)
   319→        resolved = _resolve_ts_import(spec, test_path, production_files)
   320→        if resolved:
   321→            tested.add(resolved)
   322→            continue
   323→        # Fallback: fuzzy module lookup
   324→        cleaned = spec.lstrip("./").replace("/", ".")
   325→        if cleaned in prod_by_module:
   326→            tested.add(prod_by_module[cleaned])
   327→
   328→    return tested
   329→
   330→
   331→def _map_test_to_source(
   332→    test_path: str, production_set: set[str], lang_name: str,
   333→) -> str | None:
   334→    """Try to match a test file to a production file by naming convention."""
   335→    basename = os.path.basename(test_path)
   336→    dirname = os.path.dirname(test_path)
   337→    parent = os.path.dirname(dirname)
   338→
   339→    candidates: list[str] = []
   340→
   341→    if lang_name == "python":
   342→        # test_X.py → X.py
   343→        if basename.startswith("test_"):
   344→            src = basename[5:]
   345→            candidates.append(os.path.join(dirname, src))
   346→            if parent:
   347→                candidates.append(os.path.join(parent, src))
   348→        # X_test.py → X.py
   349→        if basename.endswith("_test.py"):
   350→            src = basename[:-8] + ".py"
   351→            candidates.append(os.path.join(dirname, src))
   352→            if parent:
   353→                candidates.append(os.path.join(parent, src))
   354→    else:  # typescript
   355→        # X.test.ts → X.ts, X.test.tsx → X.tsx
   356→        for pattern in (".test.", ".spec."):
   357→            if pattern in basename:
   358→                src = basename.replace(pattern, ".")
   359→                candidates.append(os.path.join(dirname, src))
   360→                if parent:
   361→                    candidates.append(os.path.join(parent, src))
   362→        # __tests__/X.ts → ../X.ts
   363→        dir_basename = os.path.basename(dirname)
   364→        if dir_basename == "__tests__" and parent:
   365→            candidates.append(os.path.join(parent, basename))
   366→
   367→    # Fuzzy: strip test/spec suffix and try matching basename
   368→    for prod in production_set:
   369→        prod_base = os.path.basename(prod)
   370→        for c in candidates:
   371→            if os.path.basename(c) == prod_base:
   372→                # Check if the candidate path matches (same dir or parent)
   373→                if prod in production_set:
   374→                    return prod
   375→
   376→    # Direct path match
   377→    for c in candidates:
   378→        if c in production_set:
   379→            return c
   380→
   381→    return None
   382→
   383→
   384→def _naming_based_mapping(
   385→    test_files: set[str], production_files: set[str], lang_name: str,
   386→) -> set[str]:
   387→    """Map test files to production files by naming conventions."""
   388→    tested = set()
   389→    # Build basename → set of full paths for fast lookup
   390→    prod_by_basename: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   391→    for p in production_files:
   392→        bn = os.path.basename(p)
   393→        prod_by_basename.setdefault(bn, []).append(p)
   394→
   395→    for tf in test_files:
   396→        matched = _map_test_to_source(tf, production_files, lang_name)
   397→        if matched:
   398→            tested.add(matched)
   399→            continue
   400→
   401→        # Fallback: fuzzy basename matching
   402→        basename = os.path.basename(tf)
   403→        src_name = _strip_test_markers(basename, lang_name)
   404→        if src_name and src_name in prod_by_basename:
   405→            for p in prod_by_basename[src_name]:
   406→                tested.add(p)
   407→
   408→    return tested
   409→
   410→
   411→def _strip_test_markers(basename: str, lang_name: str) -> str | None:
   412→    """Strip test naming markers from a basename to get the source name."""
   413→    if lang_name == "python":
   414→        if basename.startswith("test_"):
   415→            return basename[5:]
   416→        if basename.endswith("_test.py"):
   417→            return basename[:-8] + ".py"
   418→    else:
   419→        for marker in (".test.", ".spec."):
   420→            if marker in basename:
   421→                return basename.replace(marker, ".")
   422→    return None
   423→
   424→
   425→def _transitive_coverage(
   426→    directly_tested: set[str],
   427→    graph: dict,
   428→    production_files: set[str],
   429→) -> set[str]:
   430→    """BFS from directly-tested files through dep graph imports.
   431→
   432→    Returns production files reachable from tested files (transitively covered).
   433→    """
   434→    visited = set(directly_tested)
   435→    queue = deque(directly_tested)
   436→
   437→    while queue:
   438→        current = queue.popleft()
   439→        entry = graph.get(current)
   440→        if entry is None:
   441→            continue
   442→        for imp in entry.get("imports", set()):
   443→            if imp in production_files and imp not in visited:
   444→                visited.add(imp)
   445→                queue.append(imp)
   446→
   447→    # Transitively covered = reachable but NOT directly tested
   448→    return visited - directly_tested
   449→
   450→
   451→def _strip_py_comment(line: str) -> str:
   452→    """Strip Python # comments while respecting string literals."""
   453→    in_str = None
   454→    for i, ch in enumerate(line):
   455→        if in_str:
   456→            if ch == '\\' and i + 1 < len(line):
   457→                continue  # skip escaped char (next iteration handles it)
   458→            if ch == in_str:
   459→                in_str = None
   460→        elif ch in ('"', "'"):
   461→            in_str = ch
   462→        elif ch == '#' and not in_str:
   463→            return line[:i]
   464→    return line
   465→
   466→
   467→def _analyze_test_quality(
   468→    test_files: set[str], lang_name: str,
   469→) -> dict[str, dict]:
   470→    """Analyze quality of each test file.
   471→
   472→    Returns {test_path: {"assertions": int, "mocks": int, "test_functions": int,
   473→                          "snapshots": int, "quality": str}}
   474→    """
   475→    assert_pats = PY_ASSERT_PATTERNS if lang_name == "python" else TS_ASSERT_PATTERNS
   476→    mock_pats = PY_MOCK_PATTERNS if lang_name == "python" else TS_MOCK_PATTERNS
   477→    test_func_re = PY_TEST_FUNC if lang_name == "python" else TS_TEST_FUNC
   478→
   479→    quality_map: dict[str, dict] = {}
   480→
   481→    for tf in test_files:
   482→        try:
   483→            content = Path(tf).read_text()
   484→        except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError):
   485→            continue
   486→
   487→        # Strip comments before pattern matching
   488→        if lang_name != "python":
   489→            from ..lang.typescript.detectors._smell_helpers import _strip_ts_comments
   490→            stripped = _strip_ts_comments(content)
   491→        else:
   492→            stripped = "\n".join(_strip_py_comment(line) for line in content.splitlines())
   493→
   494→        lines = stripped.splitlines()
   495→
   496→        # Use any() per line to avoid double-counting when multiple patterns
   497→        # match the same line (e.g. expect(el).toBeVisible() matching both)
   498→        assertions = sum(
   499→            1 for line in lines
   500→            if any(pat.search(line) for pat in assert_pats)
   501→        )
   502→        mocks = sum(
   503→            1 for line in lines
   504→            if any(pat.search(line) for pat in mock_pats)
   505→        )
   506→        snapshots = sum(
   507→            1 for line in lines
   508→            if any(pat.search(line) for pat in TS_SNAPSHOT_PATTERNS)
   509→        ) if lang_name != "python" else 0
   510→
   511→        test_functions = len(test_func_re.findall(stripped))
   512→
   513→        # Classify quality
   514→        if test_functions == 0:
   515→            quality = "no_tests"
   516→        elif assertions == 0:
   517→            quality = "assertion_free"
   518→        elif mocks > assertions:
   519→            quality = "over_mocked"
   520→        elif snapshots > 0 and snapshots > assertions * 0.5:
   521→            quality = "snapshot_heavy"
   522→        elif test_functions > 0 and assertions / test_functions < 1:
   523→            quality = "smoke"
   524→        elif assertions / test_functions >= 3:
   525→            quality = "thorough"
   526→        else:
   527→            quality = "adequate"
   528→
   529→        quality_map[tf] = {
   530→            "assertions": assertions,
   531→            "mocks": mocks,
   532→            "test_functions": test_functions,
   533→            "snapshots": snapshots,
   534→            "quality": quality,
   535→        }
   536→
   537→    return quality_map
   538→
   539→
   540→def _get_test_files_for_prod(
   541→    prod_file: str,
   542→    test_files: set[str],
   543→    graph: dict,
   544→    lang_name: str,
   545→) -> list[str]:
   546→    """Find which test files test a given production file."""
   547→    result = []
   548→    for tf in test_files:
   549→        entry = graph.get(tf)
   550→        if entry and prod_file in entry.get("imports", set()):
   551→            result.append(tf)
   552→            continue
   553→        # Naming match
   554→        if _map_test_to_source(tf, {prod_file}, lang_name) == prod_file:
   555→            result.append(tf)
   556→    return result
   557→
   558→
   559→def _generate_findings(
   560→    scorable: set[str],
   561→    directly_tested: set[str],
   562→    transitively_tested: set[str],
   563→    test_quality: dict[str, dict],
   564→    graph: dict,
   565→    lang_name: str,
   566→) -> list[dict]:
   567→    """Generate test coverage findings from the analysis results."""
   568→    entries: list[dict] = []
   569→
   570→    # Collect all test files for mapping
   571→    test_files = set(test_quality.keys())
   572→
   573→    for f in scorable:
   574→        loc = _file_loc(f)
   575→        ic = graph.get(f, {}).get("importer_count", 0)
   576→
   577→        if f in directly_tested:
   578→            # Check quality of the test(s) for this file
   579→            related_tests = _get_test_files_for_prod(f, test_files, graph, lang_name)
   580→            for tf in related_tests:
   581→                tq = test_quality.get(tf)
   582→                if tq is None:
   583→                    continue
   584→
   585→                if tq["quality"] == "assertion_free":
   586→                    entries.append({
   587→                        "file": f,
   588→                        "name": f"assertion_free::{os.path.basename(tf)}",
   589→                        "tier": 3,
   590→                        "confidence": "medium",
   591→                        "summary": (f"Assertion-free test: {os.path.basename(tf)} "
   592→                                    f"has {tq['test_functions']} test functions but 0 assertions"),
   593→                        "detail": {"kind": "assertion_free_test", "test_file": tf,
   594→                                   "test_functions": tq["test_functions"]},
   595→                    })
   596→                elif tq["quality"] == "smoke":
   597→                    entries.append({
   598→                        "file": f,
   599→                        "name": f"shallow::{os.path.basename(tf)}",
   600→                        "tier": 3,
   601→                        "confidence": "medium",
   602→                        "summary": (f"Shallow tests: {os.path.basename(tf)} has "
   603→                                    f"{tq['assertions']} assertions across "
   604→                                    f"{tq['test_functions']} test functions"),
   605→                        "detail": {"kind": "shallow_tests", "test_file": tf,
   606→                                   "assertions": tq["assertions"],
   607→                                   "test_functions": tq["test_functions"]},
   608→                    })
   609→                elif tq["quality"] == "over_mocked":
   610→                    entries.append({
   611→                        "file": f,
   612→                        "name": f"over_mocked::{os.path.basename(tf)}",
   613→                        "tier": 3,
   614→                        "confidence": "low",
   615→                        "summary": (f"Over-mocked tests: {os.path.basename(tf)} has "
   616→                                    f"{tq['mocks']} mocks vs {tq['assertions']} assertions"),
   617→                        "detail": {"kind": "over_mocked", "test_file": tf,
   618→                                   "mocks": tq["mocks"], "assertions": tq["assertions"]},
   619→                    })
   620→                elif tq["quality"] == "snapshot_heavy" and lang_name != "python":
   621→                    entries.append({
   622→                        "file": f,
   623→                        "name": f"snapshot_heavy::{os.path.basename(tf)}",
   624→                        "tier": 3,
   625→                        "confidence": "low",
   626→                        "summary": (f"Snapshot-heavy tests: {os.path.basename(tf)} has "
   627→                                    f"{tq['snapshots']} snapshots vs {tq['assertions']} assertions"),
   628→                        "detail": {"kind": "snapshot_heavy", "test_file": tf,
   629→                                   "snapshots": tq["snapshots"],
   630→                                   "assertions": tq["assertions"]},
   631→                    })
   632→
   633→        elif f in transitively_tested:
   634→            entries.append({
   635→                "file": f,
   636→                "name": "transitive_only",
   637→                "tier": 2 if ic >= 10 else 3,
   638→                "confidence": "medium",
   639→                "summary": (f"No direct tests ({loc} LOC, {ic} importers) "
   640→                            f"— covered only via imports from tested modules"),
   641→                "detail": {"kind": "transitive_only", "loc": loc, "importer_count": ic},
   642→            })
   643→
   644→        else:
   645→            # Untested
   646→            if ic >= 10:
   647→                entries.append({
   648→                    "file": f,
   649→                    "name": "untested_critical",
   650→                    "tier": 2,
   651→                    "confidence": "high",
   652→                    "summary": (f"Untested critical module ({loc} LOC, {ic} importers) "
   653→                                f"— high blast radius"),
   654→                    "detail": {"kind": "untested_critical", "loc": loc, "importer_count": ic},
   655→                })
   656→            else:
   657→                entries.append({
   658→                    "file": f,
   659→                    "name": "untested_module",
   660→                    "tier": 3,
   661→                    "confidence": "high",
   662→                    "summary": f"Untested module ({loc} LOC, {ic} importers)",
   663→                    "detail": {"kind": "untested_module", "loc": loc, "importer_count": ic},
   664→                })
   665→
   666→    return entries
   667→

1→"""Contextual reminders with decay."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from ._constants import STRUCTURAL_MERGE, _FEEDBACK_URL, _REMINDER_DECAY_THRESHOLD
     6→
     7→
     8→def _compute_fp_rates(findings: dict) -> dict[tuple[str, str], float]:
     9→    """Compute false_positive rate per (detector, zone) from historical findings.
    10→
    11→    Returns rates only for combinations with >= 5 total findings and FP rate > 0.
    12→    """
    13→    counts: dict[tuple[str, str], dict[str, int]] = {}
    14→    for f in findings.values():
    15→        det = f.get("detector", "unknown")
    16→        if det in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
    17→            det = "structural"
    18→        zone = f.get("zone", "production")
    19→        key = (det, zone)
    20→        if key not in counts:
    21→            counts[key] = {"total": 0, "fp": 0}
    22→        counts[key]["total"] += 1
    23→        if f.get("status") == "false_positive":
    24→            counts[key]["fp"] += 1
    25→
    26→    rates = {}
    27→    for key, c in counts.items():
    28→        if c["total"] >= 5 and c["fp"] > 0:
    29→            rates[key] = c["fp"] / c["total"]
    30→    return rates
    31→
    32→
    33→def _compute_reminders(state: dict, lang: str | None,
    34→                       phase: str, debt: dict, actions: list[dict],
    35→                       dimensions: dict, badge: dict,
    36→                       command: str | None) -> list[dict]:
    37→    """Compute context-specific reminders, suppressing those shown too many times."""
    38→    reminders = []
    39→    obj_strict = state.get("objective_strict")
    40→    reminder_history = state.get("reminder_history", {})
    41→
    42→    # 1. Auto-fixers available
    43→    if lang != "python":
    44→        auto_fix_actions = [a for a in actions if a.get("type") == "auto_fix"]
    45→        if auto_fix_actions:
    46→            total = sum(a.get("count", 0) for a in auto_fix_actions)
    47→            if total > 0:
    48→                first_cmd = auto_fix_actions[0].get("command", "desloppify fix <fixer> --dry-run")
    49→                reminders.append({
    50→                    "type": "auto_fixers_available",
    51→                    "message": f"{total} findings are auto-fixable. Run `{first_cmd}`.",
    52→                    "command": first_cmd,
    53→                })
    54→
    55→    # 2. Rescan needed — only after fix or resolve, not passive queries
    56→    if command in ("fix", "resolve", "ignore"):
    57→        reminders.append({
    58→            "type": "rescan_needed",
    59→            "message": "Rescan to verify — cascading effects may create new findings.",
    60→            "command": "desloppify scan",
    61→        })
    62→
    63→    # 3. Badge recommendation (strict >= 90 and README doesn't have it)
    64→    if obj_strict is not None and obj_strict >= 90:
    65→        if badge.get("generated") and not badge.get("in_readme"):
    66→            reminders.append({
    67→                "type": "badge_recommendation",
    68→                "message": ('Score is above 90! Add the scorecard to your README: '
    69→                            '<img src="scorecard.png" width="400">'),
    70→                "command": None,
    71→            })
    72→
    73→    # 4. Wontfix debt growing
    74→    if debt.get("trend") == "growing":
    75→        reminders.append({
    76→            "type": "wontfix_growing",
    77→            "message": "Wontfix debt is growing. Review stale decisions: `desloppify show --status wontfix`.",
    78→            "command": "desloppify show --status wontfix",
    79→        })
    80→
    81→    # 5. Stagnant dimensions — be specific about what to try
    82→    for dim in dimensions.get("stagnant_dimensions", []):
    83→        strict = dim.get("strict", 0)
    84→        if strict >= 99:
    85→            msg = (f"{dim['name']} has been at {strict}% for {dim['stuck_scans']} scans. "
    86→                   f"The remaining items may be worth marking as wontfix if they're intentional.")
    87→        else:
    88→            msg = (f"{dim['name']} has been stuck at {strict}% for {dim['stuck_scans']} scans. "
    89→                   f"Try tackling it from a different angle — run `desloppify next` to find the right entry point.")
    90→        reminders.append({
    91→            "type": "stagnant_nudge",
    92→            "message": msg,
    93→            "command": None,
    94→        })
    95→
    96→    # 6. Dry-run first (when top action is auto_fix)
    97→    if actions and actions[0].get("type") == "auto_fix":
    98→        reminders.append({
    99→            "type": "dry_run_first",
   100→            "message": "Always --dry-run first, review changes, then apply.",
   101→            "command": None,
   102→        })
   103→
   104→    # 7. Zone classification awareness (reminder decay handles repetition)
   105→    zone_dist = state.get("zone_distribution")
   106→    if zone_dist:
   107→        non_prod = sum(v for k, v in zone_dist.items() if k != "production")
   108→        if non_prod > 0:
   109→            total = sum(zone_dist.values())
   110→            parts = [f"{v} {k}" for k, v in sorted(zone_dist.items())
   111→                     if k != "production" and v > 0]
   112→            reminders.append({
   113→                "type": "zone_classification",
   114→                "message": (f"{non_prod} of {total} files classified as non-production "
   115→                            f"({', '.join(parts)}). "
   116→                            f"Override with `desloppify zone set <file> production` "
   117→                            f"if any are misclassified."),
   118→                "command": "desloppify zone show",
   119→            })
   120→
   121→    # 8. Zone-aware FP rate calibration reminders
   122→    fp_rates = _compute_fp_rates(state.get("findings", {}))
   123→    for (detector, zone), rate in fp_rates.items():
   124→        if rate > 0.3:
   125→            pct = round(rate * 100)
   126→            reminders.append({
   127→                "type": f"fp_calibration_{detector}_{zone}",
   128→                "message": (f"{pct}% of {detector} findings in {zone} zone are false positives. "
   129→                            f"Consider reviewing detection rules for {zone} files."),
   130→                "command": None,
   131→            })
   132→
   133→    # 9. Review staleness — nudge when oldest review is past max age
   134→    review_cache = state.get("review_cache", {})
   135→    if review_cache.get("files"):
   136→        from datetime import datetime as _dt, timezone as _tz
   137→        try:
   138→            oldest_str = min(
   139→                f["reviewed_at"] for f in review_cache["files"].values()
   140→                if f.get("reviewed_at")
   141→            )
   142→            oldest = _dt.fromisoformat(oldest_str)
   143→            age_days = (_dt.now(_tz.utc) - oldest).days
   144→            if age_days > 30:
   145→                reminders.append({
   146→                    "type": "review_stale",
   147→                    "message": (f"Design review is {age_days} days old \u2014 "
   148→                                f"run: desloppify review --prepare"),
   149→                    "command": "desloppify review --prepare",
   150→                })
   151→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   152→            pass  # Malformed dates — skip
   153→
   154→    # 10. Feedback nudge — encourage filing issues for tool improvement
   155→    #    Scan-only so the 3 decay slots are spread across scan cycles, not
   156→    #    burned in a single scan→show→fix session.  All variants share one
   157→    #    decay key ("feedback_nudge") so the total is 3, not 3 per variant.
   158→    scan_count = len(state.get("scan_history", []))
   159→    if scan_count >= 2 and command == "scan":
   160→        high_fp_dets = [det for (det, _zone), rate in fp_rates.items() if rate > 0.3]
   161→        if high_fp_dets:
   162→            nudge_msg = (
   163→                f"Some detectors have high false-positive rates ({', '.join(high_fp_dets)}). "
   164→                f"If patterns are being misclassified, file an issue at "
   165→                f"{_FEEDBACK_URL} with the file and expected behavior — "
   166→                f"it helps calibrate detection for everyone.")
   167→        elif phase == "stagnation":
   168→            nudge_msg = (
   169→                f"Score has plateaued — if you suspect desloppify is missing patterns "
   170→                f"or not capturing something it should, file an issue at "
   171→                f"{_FEEDBACK_URL} describing what you expected. "
   172→                f"Gaps in detection are a common cause of stagnation.")
   173→        else:
   174→            nudge_msg = (
   175→                f"If you notice patterns desloppify doesn't detect, false positives, "
   176→                f"or findings that seem off, file an issue at {_FEEDBACK_URL} — "
   177→                f"include the file and what you expected. It helps improve the tool.")
   178→        reminders.append({
   179→            "type": "feedback_nudge",
   180→            "message": nudge_msg,
   181→            "command": None,
   182→        })
   183→
   184→    # Apply decay: suppress reminders shown >= threshold times
   185→    filtered = []
   186→    for r in reminders:
   187→        count = reminder_history.get(r["type"], 0)
   188→        if count < _REMINDER_DECAY_THRESHOLD:
   189→            filtered.append(r)
   190→
   191→    # Compute updated reminder history (returned via narrative result, not mutated here)
   192→    updated_history = dict(reminder_history)
   193→    for r in filtered:
   194→        updated_history[r["type"]] = updated_history.get(r["type"], 0) + 1
   195→
   196→    return filtered, updated_history
   197→

1→"""Phase detection and milestone computation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→
     6→def _detect_phase(history: list[dict], obj_strict: float | None) -> str:
     7→    """Detect project phase from scan history trajectory."""
     8→    if not history:
     9→        return "first_scan"
    10→
    11→    if len(history) == 1:
    12→        return "first_scan"
    13→
    14→    strict = obj_strict
    15→    if strict is None and history:
    16→        strict = history[-1].get("objective_strict")
    17→
    18→    # Check regression: strict dropped from previous scan
    19→    if len(history) >= 2:
    20→        prev = history[-2].get("objective_strict")
    21→        curr = history[-1].get("objective_strict")
    22→        if prev is not None and curr is not None and curr < prev - 0.5:
    23→            return "regression"
    24→
    25→    # Check stagnation: strict unchanged ±0.5 for 3+ scans
    26→    if len(history) >= 3:
    27→        recent = [h.get("objective_strict") for h in history[-3:]]
    28→        if all(r is not None for r in recent):
    29→            spread = max(recent) - min(recent)
    30→            if spread <= 0.5:
    31→                return "stagnation"
    32→
    33→    # Early momentum: scans 2-5 with score rising — check BEFORE score thresholds
    34→    # so early projects get motivational framing even if score is already high
    35→    if len(history) <= 5 and len(history) >= 2:
    36→        first = history[0].get("objective_strict")
    37→        last = history[-1].get("objective_strict")
    38→        if first is not None and last is not None and last > first:
    39→            return "early_momentum"
    40→
    41→    if strict is not None:
    42→        if strict > 93:
    43→            return "maintenance"
    44→        if strict > 80:
    45→            return "refinement"
    46→
    47→    return "middle_grind"
    48→
    49→
    50→def _detect_milestone(state: dict, diff: dict | None,
    51→                      history: list[dict]) -> str | None:
    52→    """Detect notable milestones worth celebrating."""
    53→    obj_strict = state.get("objective_strict")
    54→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
    55→
    56→    # Check T1 clear
    57→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})
    58→    t1_open = by_tier.get("1", {}).get("open", 0)
    59→    t2_open = by_tier.get("2", {}).get("open", 0)
    60→
    61→    if len(history) >= 2:
    62→        prev_strict = history[-2].get("objective_strict")
    63→        if prev_strict is not None and obj_strict is not None:
    64→            # Crossed 90
    65→            if prev_strict < 90 and obj_strict >= 90:
    66→                return "Crossed 90% strict!"
    67→            # Crossed 80
    68→            if prev_strict < 80 and obj_strict >= 80:
    69→                return "Crossed 80% strict!"
    70→
    71→    if t1_open == 0 and t2_open == 0:
    72→        # Check if there were T1/T2 items before
    73→        total_t1 = sum(by_tier.get("1", {}).values())
    74→        total_t2 = sum(by_tier.get("2", {}).values())
    75→        if total_t1 + total_t2 > 0:
    76→            return "All T1 and T2 items cleared!"
    77→
    78→    if t1_open == 0:
    79→        total_t1 = sum(by_tier.get("1", {}).values())
    80→        if total_t1 > 0:
    81→            return "All T1 items cleared!"
    82→
    83→    if stats.get("open", 0) == 0 and stats.get("total", 0) > 0:
    84→        return "Zero open findings!"
    85→
    86→    return None
    87→

1→"""Action computation and tool inventory."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from ._constants import DETECTOR_TOOLS
     6→
     7→
     8→def _compute_actions(by_det: dict[str, int], dim_scores: dict, state: dict,
     9→                     debt: dict, lang: str | None) -> list[dict]:
    10→    """Compute prioritized action list with tool mapping."""
    11→    from ..scoring import merge_potentials, compute_score_impact, get_dimension_for_detector
    12→
    13→    potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
    14→    actions = []
    15→    priority = 0
    16→
    17→    def _impact_for(det: str, count: int) -> float:
    18→        if not potentials or not dim_scores:
    19→            return 0.0
    20→        return compute_score_impact(
    21→            {k: {"score": v["score"], "tier": v.get("tier", 3),
    22→                  "detectors": v.get("detectors", {})}
    23→             for k, v in dim_scores.items()},
    24→            potentials, det, count)
    25→
    26→    def _dim_name_for(det: str) -> str:
    27→        dim = get_dimension_for_detector(det)
    28→        return dim.name if dim else "Unknown"
    29→
    30→    # Auto-fixable actions (or manual-fix for Python)
    31→    for det, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
    32→        if tool_info["action_type"] != "auto_fix":
    33→            continue
    34→        count = by_det.get(det, 0)
    35→        if count == 0:
    36→            continue
    37→
    38→        impact = _impact_for(det, count)
    39→
    40→        # Python has no auto-fixers — suggest manual fix instead
    41→        if lang == "python":
    42→            priority += 1
    43→            actions.append({
    44→                "priority": priority,
    45→                "type": "manual_fix",
    46→                "detector": det,
    47→                "count": count,
    48→                "description": f"{count} {det} findings — fix manually",
    49→                "command": f"desloppify show {det} --status open",
    50→                "impact": round(impact, 1),
    51→                "dimension": _dim_name_for(det),
    52→            })
    53→            continue
    54→
    55→        for fixer in tool_info["fixers"]:
    56→            priority += 1
    57→            actions.append({
    58→                "priority": priority,
    59→                "type": "auto_fix",
    60→                "detector": det,
    61→                "count": count,
    62→                "description": f"{count} {det} findings — auto-fixable",
    63→                "command": f"desloppify fix {fixer} --dry-run",
    64→                "impact": round(impact, 1),
    65→                "dimension": _dim_name_for(det),
    66→            })
    67→            break  # One action per detector, listing first fixer
    68→
    69→    # Reorganize actions
    70→    for det, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
    71→        if tool_info["action_type"] != "reorganize":
    72→            continue
    73→        count = by_det.get(det, 0)
    74→        if count == 0:
    75→            continue
    76→
    77→        impact = _impact_for(det, count)
    78→        priority += 1
    79→        guidance = tool_info.get("guidance", "restructure with move")
    80→        actions.append({
    81→            "priority": priority,
    82→            "type": "reorganize",
    83→            "detector": det,
    84→            "count": count,
    85→            "description": f"{count} {det} findings — {guidance}",
    86→            "command": f"desloppify show {det} --status open",
    87→            "impact": round(impact, 1),
    88→            "dimension": _dim_name_for(det),
    89→        })
    90→
    91→    # Refactor actions
    92→    for det, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
    93→        if tool_info["action_type"] not in ("refactor", "manual_fix"):
    94→            continue
    95→        count = by_det.get(det, 0)
    96→        if count == 0:
    97→            continue
    98→
    99→        impact = _impact_for(det, count)
   100→        priority += 1
   101→        actions.append({
   102→            "priority": priority,
   103→            "type": tool_info["action_type"],
   104→            "detector": det,
   105→            "count": count,
   106→            "description": f"{count} {det} findings — {tool_info.get('guidance', 'manual fix')}",
   107→            "command": f"desloppify show {det} --status open",
   108→            "impact": round(impact, 1),
   109→            "dimension": _dim_name_for(det),
   110→        })
   111→
   112→    # Debt review action
   113→    if debt.get("overall_gap", 0) > 2.0:
   114→        priority += 1
   115→        actions.append({
   116→            "priority": priority,
   117→            "type": "debt_review",
   118→            "detector": None,
   119→            "description": f"{debt['overall_gap']} pts of wontfix debt — review stale decisions",
   120→            "command": "desloppify show --status wontfix",
   121→            "gap": debt["overall_gap"],
   122→        })
   123→
   124→    # Sort by impact descending, auto_fix first
   125→    type_order = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3, "debt_review": 4}
   126→    actions.sort(key=lambda a: (type_order.get(a["type"], 9), -a.get("impact", 0)))
   127→    for i, a in enumerate(actions):
   128→        a["priority"] = i + 1
   129→
   130→    return actions
   131→
   132→
   133→def _compute_tools(by_det: dict[str, int], lang: str | None,
   134→                   badge: dict) -> dict:
   135→    """Compute available tools inventory for the current context."""
   136→    # Available fixers (only those with >0 open findings)
   137→    fixers = []
   138→    if lang != "python":
   139→        for det, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
   140→            if tool_info["action_type"] != "auto_fix":
   141→                continue
   142→            count = by_det.get(det, 0)
   143→            if count == 0:
   144→                continue
   145→            for fixer in tool_info["fixers"]:
   146→                fixers.append({
   147→                    "name": fixer,
   148→                    "detector": det,
   149→                    "open_count": count,
   150→                    "command": f"desloppify fix {fixer} --dry-run",
   151→                })
   152→
   153→    # Move tool relevance
   154→    org_issues = sum(by_det.get(d, 0) for d in
   155→                     ["orphaned", "flat_dirs", "naming", "single_use", "coupling", "cycles"])
   156→    move_reasons = []
   157→    if by_det.get("orphaned", 0):
   158→        move_reasons.append(f"{by_det['orphaned']} orphaned files")
   159→    if by_det.get("coupling", 0):
   160→        move_reasons.append(f"{by_det['coupling']} coupling violations")
   161→    if by_det.get("single_use", 0):
   162→        move_reasons.append(f"{by_det['single_use']} single-use files")
   163→    if by_det.get("flat_dirs", 0):
   164→        move_reasons.append(f"{by_det['flat_dirs']} flat directories")
   165→    if by_det.get("naming", 0):
   166→        move_reasons.append(f"{by_det['naming']} naming issues")
   167→
   168→    return {
   169→        "fixers": fixers,
   170→        "move": {
   171→            "available": True,
   172→            "relevant": org_issues > 0,
   173→            "reason": " + ".join(move_reasons) if move_reasons else None,
   174→            "usage": "desloppify move <source> <dest> [--dry-run]",
   175→        },
   176→        "plan": {
   177→            "command": "desloppify plan",
   178→            "description": "Generate prioritized markdown cleanup plan",
   179→        },
   180→        "badge": badge,
   181→    }
   182→

    87→// List public resources (available to all users)
    88→export const useListPublicResources = (type: ResourceType) => {
    89→    return useQuery<Resource[], Error>({
    90→        queryKey: [...queryKeys.resources.public(type), 'v2'],
    91→        queryFn: async () => {
    92→            
    93→            // Manual pagination to bypass 1000 limit
    94→            let allData: Resource[] = [];
    95→            let page = 0;
    96→            const pageSize = 1000;
    97→            let hasMore = true;
    98→
    99→            while (hasMore) {
   100→                const { data, error } = await supabase
   101→                    .from('resources')
   102→                    .select('*')
   103→                    .eq('type', type)
   104→                    .eq('is_public', true)
   105→                    .range(page * pageSize, (page + 1) * pageSize - 1);
   106→                
   107→                if (error) {
   108→                    handleError(error, { context: 'useListPublicResources', showToast: false, logData: { type } });
   109→                    throw error;
   110→                }
   111→                
   112→                if (data) {
   113→                    allData = [...allData, ...data];
   114→                    if (data.length < pageSize) {
   115→                        hasMore = false;
   116→                    } else {
   117→                        page++;
   118→                    }
   119→                } else {
   120→                    hasMore = false;
   121→                }
   122→                
   123→                // Safety limit to prevent infinite loops
   124→                if (allData.length >= 20000) {
   125→                    hasMore = false;
   126→                }
   127→            }
   128→            
   129→            return allData;
   130→        },
   131→        // Use static preset - public resources change infrequently
   132→        ...QUERY_PRESETS.static,
   133→        staleTime: 15 * 60 * 1000, // Override: 15 minutes (public resources are very stable)
   134→        gcTime: 30 * 60 * 1000, // Keep in cache longer
   135→    });
   136→};

   153→            while (hasMore) {

   155→                    .from('resources')

   157→                    .eq('user_id', user.id)
   158→                    .eq('type', type)
   159→                    .range(page * pageSize, (page + 1) * pageSize - 1);
   160→

   162→
   163→                if (data) {

   165→                    if (data.length < pageSize) {

   173→                
   174→                 // Safety limit
   175→                 if (allData.length >= 20000) break;
   176→            }
   177→
   178→            return allData;
   179→        },
   180→        // Use static preset - user resources change only via mutations (which invalidate)
   181→        ...QUERY_PRESETS.static,

   231→
   232→export const useUpdateResource = () => {
   233→    const queryClient = useQueryClient();
   234→    return useMutation<Resource, Error, UpdateResourceArgs>({
   235→        mutationFn: async ({ id, metadata }) => {
   236→
   237→            const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
   238→            if (!user) {
   239→                const err = new Error('Not authenticated');
   240→                handleError(err, { context: 'useUpdateResource', showToast: false });
   241→                throw err;
   242→            }
   243→
   244→            // First, let's check if the resource exists at all (without user_id filter)
   245→            const { data: resourceCheck } = await supabase
   246→                .from('resources')
   247→                .select('id, user_id, type')
   248→                .eq('id', id)
   249→                .maybeSingle();
   250→            
   251→            // Now verify the resource exists and belongs to the user
   252→            const { data: existingResource, error: checkError } = await supabase
   253→                .from('resources')
   254→                .select('id, user_id, type')
   255→                .eq('id', id)
   256→                .eq('user_id', user.id)
   257→                .maybeSingle();
   258→            
   259→            if (checkError) {
   260→                handleError(checkError, { context: 'useUpdateResource', showToast: false });
   261→                throw new Error(`Failed to verify resource: ${checkError.message}`);
   262→            }
   263→            
   264→            if (!existingResource) {
   265→                handleError(new Error('Resource not found or access denied'), { context: 'useUpdateResource', showToast: false, logData: {
   266→                    id,
   267→                    userId: user.id,
   268→                    resourceExists: !!resourceCheck,
   269→                    resourceOwner: resourceCheck?.user_id
   270→                }});
   271→
   272→                // Provide a more specific error message
   273→                if (resourceCheck && resourceCheck.user_id !== user.id) {
   274→                    throw new Error('This resource belongs to another user');
   275→                }
   276→                throw new Error('Resource not found or you do not have permission to update it');
   277→            }
   278→            
   279→            // Extract is_public from metadata for the column
   280→            const isPublic = 'is_public' in metadata ? Boolean((metadata as Record<string, unknown>).is_public) : false;
   281→            
   282→            // Now perform the update
   283→            const { data, error } = await supabase
   284→                .from('resources')
   285→                .update({ metadata, is_public: isPublic })
   286→                .eq('id', id)
   287→                .eq('user_id', user.id)
   288→                .select()
   289→                .maybeSingle();
   290→            
   291→            if (error) {
   292→                handleError(error, { context: 'useUpdateResource', showToast: false, logData: {
   293→                    code: error.code,
   294→                    details: error.details,
   295→                    hint: error.hint
   296→                }});
   297→                throw error;
   298→            }
   299→            
   300→            if (!data) {
   301→                handleError(new Error('Update succeeded but no data returned'), { context: 'useUpdateResource', showToast: false });
   302→                // If update succeeded but no data returned, fetch it separately
   303→                const { data: fetchedData, error: fetchError } = await supabase
   304→                    .from('resources')
   305→                    .select('*')
   306→                    .eq('id', id)
   307→                    .maybeSingle();
   308→                
   309→                if (fetchError || !fetchedData) {
   310→                    handleError(fetchError || new Error('No data returned'), { context: 'useUpdateResource', showToast: false });
   311→                    throw new Error('Update may have succeeded but failed to fetch updated resource');
   312→                }
   313→                
   314→                return fetchedData;
   315→            }
   316→            
   317→            return data;
   318→        },
   319→        onSuccess: (data) => {
   320→            // Invalidate both v2 query keys to ensure fresh data
   321→            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [...queryKeys.resources.all, data.type, 'v2'] });
   322→            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [...queryKeys.resources.public(data.type), 'v2'] });
   323→            // Also invalidate without v2 for backwards compatibility
   324→            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [...queryKeys.resources.all, data.type] });
   325→            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED](data.type) });
   326→            // Invalidate specific-resources queries that include this resource ID
   327→            // Using predicate to find any query that contains this resource ID
   328→            queryClient.invalidateQueries({
   329→                predicate: (query) => {
   330→                    const key = query.queryKey;
   331→                    if (key[0] === 'specific-resources' && typeof key[1] === 'string') {
   332→                        return key[1].includes(data.id);
   333→                    }
   334→                    return false;
   335→                }
   336→            });
   337→        },
   338→        onError: (error) => {
   339→            handleError(error, { context: 'useUpdateResource', toastTitle: 'Failed to update resource' });
   340→        },
   341→    });
   342→};
   343→
   344→// Delete a resource
   345→export const useDeleteResource = () => {
   346→    const queryClient = useQueryClient();
   347→    return useMutation<void, Error, { id: string, type: ResourceType }>({
   348→        mutationFn: async ({ id }) => {
   349→            const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
   350→            if (!user) throw new Error('Not authenticated');
   351→
   352→            const { error } = await supabase
   353→                .from('resources')
   354→                .delete()
   355→                .eq('id', id)
   356→                .eq('user_id', user.id);
   357→
   358→            if (error) throw error;
   359→        },
   360→        onSuccess: (_data, variables) => {
   361→            // Remove the individual resource cache entry
   362→            queryClient.removeQueries({ [REDACTED](variables.id) });
   363→            // Also invalidate the list queries
   364→            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [...queryKeys.resources.all, variables.type] });
   365→            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED](variables.type) });
   366→        },
   367→        onError: (error) => {
   368→            handleError(error, { context: 'useDeleteResource', toastTitle: 'Failed to delete resource' });
   369→        },
   370→    });
   371→};

1→/**
     2→ * Segment Settings Utilities
     3→ *
     4→ * Clean merge logic for per-segment video generation settings.
     5→ *
     6→ * Priority (highest to lowest):
     7→ * 1. pairMetadata (pair-specific settings from shot_generations.metadata)
     8→ * 2. shotBatchSettings (shot-level defaults from shots.settings)
     9→ * 3. defaults (hardcoded fallbacks)
    10→ *
    11→ * Note: Shot settings inheritance (from previous shot) is handled separately
    12→ * when a new shot is created - see shotSettingsInheritance.ts
    13→ *
    14→ * Key invariants:
    15→ * - Basic mode = no phase_config (always cleared)
    16→ * - New format (pair_X fields at root) takes precedence over legacy (user_overrides.X)
    17→ *
    18→ * Migration/conversion layer (PairMetadata, buildMetadataUpdate,
    19→ * extractSettingsFromParams) lives in segmentSettingsMigration.ts.
    20→ */
    21→
    22→import { PhaseConfig, DEFAULT_PHASE_CONFIG, DEFAULT_VACE_PHASE_CONFIG } from '@/shared/types/phaseConfig';
    23→import type { ActiveLora } from '@/shared/hooks/useLoraManager';
    24→import type { StructureVideoConfig } from '@/shared/lib/tasks/travelBetweenImages';
    25→
    26→// =============================================================================
    27→// BUILT-IN PRESETS
    28→// =============================================================================
    29→
    30→export interface BuiltinPreset {
    31→  id: string;
    32→  metadata: {
    33→    name: string;
    34→    description: string;
    35→    phaseConfig: PhaseConfig;
    36→    generationTypeMode: 'i2v' | 'vace';
    37→  };
    38→}
    39→
    40→// Use the same IDs as the batch view (MotionControl.tsx) so shot defaults
    41→// are recognized as known presets when inherited by the segment form.
    42→const BUILTIN_I2V_PRESET_ID = '__builtin_default_i2v__';
    43→const BUILTIN_VACE_PRESET_ID = '__builtin_default_vace__';
    44→
    45→export const BUILTIN_I2V_PRESET: BuiltinPreset = {
    46→  id: BUILTIN_I2V_PRESET_ID,
    47→  metadata: {
    48→    name: 'Basic',
    49→    description: 'Standard I2V generation',
    50→    phaseConfig: DEFAULT_PHASE_CONFIG,
    51→    generationTypeMode: 'i2v',
    52→  }
    53→};
    54→
    55→export const BUILTIN_VACE_PRESET: BuiltinPreset = {
    56→  id: BUILTIN_VACE_PRESET_ID,
    57→  metadata: {
    58→    name: 'Basic',
    59→    description: 'Standard VACE generation with structure video',
    60→    phaseConfig: DEFAULT_VACE_PHASE_CONFIG,
    61→    generationTypeMode: 'vace',
    62→  }
    63→};
    64→
    65→// Featured preset IDs from database (shown after built-in default)
    66→export const SEGMENT_I2V_FEATURED_PRESET_IDS: string[] = [
    67→  'e1aad8bf-add9-4d7b-883b-d67d424028c4',
    68→  '18b879a5-1251-41dc-b263-613358ced541',
    69→];
    70→
    71→export const SEGMENT_VACE_FEATURED_PRESET_IDS: string[] = [
    72→  'd72377eb-6d57-4af1-80a3-9b629da28a47',
    73→];
    74→
    75→// =============================================================================
    76→// HELPERS
    77→// =============================================================================
    78→
    79→/**
    80→ * Result of computing a defaultable field's display state.
    81→ */
    82→interface DefaultableFieldResult {
    83→  /** Whether the field is currently showing the default value */
    84→  isUsingDefault: boolean;
    85→  /** The value to display in the field */
    86→  displayValue: string;
    87→}
    88→
    89→/**
    90→ * Compute display state for a field that can fall back to a default value.
    91→ *
    92→ * Semantics:
    93→ * - `undefined` = no local value set, use default (show badge)
    94→ * - `''` (empty string) = user explicitly cleared, show empty (no badge)
    95→ * - `'value'` = user set a value, show it (no badge)
    96→ *
    97→ * @param localValue - The current local/settings value (may be undefined)
    98→ * @param defaultValue - The fallback value from shot defaults
    99→ * @param hasDbOverride - Whether there's a saved override in the database (optional)
   100→ */
   101→export function getDefaultableField(
   102→  localValue: string | undefined,
   103→  defaultValue: string | undefined,
   104→  hasDbOverride?: boolean
   105→): DefaultableFieldResult {
   106→  // Key insight: check for `undefined` specifically, not falsiness
   107→  // Empty string '' means user explicitly cleared - don't show default
   108→  const isUsingDefault = localValue === undefined && (
   109→    hasDbOverride !== undefined
   110→      ? !hasDbOverride && defaultValue !== undefined
   111→      : defaultValue !== undefined
   112→  );
   113→
   114→  return {
   115→    isUsingDefault,
   116→    displayValue: isUsingDefault ? (defaultValue ?? '') : (localValue ?? ''),
   117→  };
   118→}
   119→
   120→/**
   121→ * Detect generation mode from model name.
   122→ */
   123→export function detectGenerationMode(modelName?: string): 'i2v' | 'vace' {
   124→  if (!modelName) return 'i2v';
   125→  return modelName.toLowerCase().includes('vace') ? 'vace' : 'i2v';
   126→}
   127→
   128→// =============================================================================
   129→// CONTROLLED FORM INTERFACE
   130→// =============================================================================
   131→
   132→/**
   133→ * Complete segment settings for the controlled form.
   134→ * This is the single source of truth passed to SegmentSettingsForm.
   135→ */
   136→export interface SegmentSettings {
   137→  // Prompts
   138→  prompt: string;
   139→  negativePrompt: string;
   140→  // Text before/after prompts (merged into final prompt when generating)
   141→  textBeforePrompts?: string;
   142→  textAfterPrompts?: string;
   143→  // Motion
   144→  motionMode: 'basic' | 'advanced';
   145→  amountOfMotion: number; // 0-100 (UI scale)
   146→  // Phase config (only when motionMode is advanced)
   147→  phaseConfig: PhaseConfig | undefined;
   148→  selectedPhasePresetId: string | null;
   149→  // LoRAs
   150→  loras: ActiveLora[];
   151→  // Video settings
   152→  numFrames: number;
   153→  randomSeed: boolean;
   154→  seed?: number;
   155→  // Variant behavior
   156→  makePrimaryVariant: boolean;
   157→  // Structure video overrides (only when segment has structure video)
   158→  structureMotionStrength?: number; // 0-2 scale
   159→  structureTreatment?: 'adjust' | 'clip';
   160→  structureUni3cEndPercent?: number; // 0-1 scale
   161→}
   162→
   163→
   164→// =============================================================================
   165→// DATA SOURCE TYPES
   166→// =============================================================================
   167→
   168→export interface ShotBatchSettings {
   169→  amountOfMotion?: number;
   170→  motionMode?: 'basic' | 'advanced';
   171→  selectedLoras?: ActiveLora[];
   172→  phaseConfig?: PhaseConfig;
   173→  prompt?: string;
   174→  negativePrompt?: string;
   175→}
   176→
   177→// Strip mode field from phase config (backend determines mode from model)
   178→export function stripModeFromPhaseConfig(config: PhaseConfig): PhaseConfig {
   179→  const { mode, ...rest } = config as PhaseConfig & { mode?: string };
   180→  return rest as PhaseConfig;
   181→}
   182→
   183→// =============================================================================
   184→// TASK PARAMS BUILDER
   185→// =============================================================================
   186→
   187→/**
   188→ * Build task params from segment settings.
   189→ * Used by parent components to create generation tasks.
   190→ */
   191→export function buildTaskParams(
   192→  settings: SegmentSettings,
   193→  context: {
   194→    projectId: string;
   195→    shotId?: string;
   196→    generationId?: string;
   197→    childGenerationId?: string;
   198→    segmentIndex: number;
   199→    startImageUrl: string;
   200→    endImageUrl?: string; // Optional for trailing segments (single-image-to-video)
   201→    startImageGenerationId?: string;
   202→    endImageGenerationId?: string;
   203→    pairShotGenerationId?: string;
   204→    startImageVariantId?: string;
   205→    endImageVariantId?: string;
   206→    projectResolution?: string;
   207→    // Optional enhanced prompt (AI-enhanced version, kept separate from base_prompt)
   208→    enhancedPrompt?: string;
   209→    // Structure video config for this segment (from shot timeline data)
   210→    structureVideo?: StructureVideoConfig | null;
   211→  }
   212→): Record<string, unknown> {
   213→  // Build structure_videos array if we have a structure video for this segment
   214→  const structureVideos = context.structureVideo ? [context.structureVideo] : undefined;
   215→
   216→  // Detect trailing segment: no end image means single-image-to-video (last segment)
   217→  const isTrailingSegment = !context.endImageUrl;
   218→
   219→  return {
   220→    project_id: context.projectId,
   221→    shot_id: context.shotId,
   222→    parent_generation_id: context.generationId,
   223→    child_generation_id: context.childGenerationId,
   224→    segment_index: context.segmentIndex,
   225→    start_image_url: context.startImageUrl,
   226→    end_image_url: context.endImageUrl,
   227→    // For trailing segments (no end image), mark as last segment so worker uses I2V mode
   228→    is_last_segment: isTrailingSegment,
   229→    start_image_generation_id: context.startImageGenerationId,
   230→    end_image_generation_id: context.endImageGenerationId,
   231→    pair_shot_generation_id: context.pairShotGenerationId,
   232→    start_image_variant_id: context.startImageVariantId,
   233→    end_image_variant_id: context.endImageVariantId,
   234→    // Settings
   235→    base_prompt: settings.prompt,
   236→    negative_prompt: settings.negativePrompt,
   237→    // Enhanced prompt (AI-enhanced version) - worker should prefer this over base_prompt when present
   238→    ...(context.enhancedPrompt && { enhanced_prompt: context.enhancedPrompt }),
   239→    num_frames: settings.numFrames,
   240→    random_seed: settings.randomSeed,
   241→    seed: settings.seed,
   242→    amount_of_motion: settings.amountOfMotion / 100, // Convert 0-100 to 0-1
   243→    motion_mode: settings.motionMode,
   244→    phase_config: settings.motionMode === 'basic' && !settings.selectedPhasePresetId ? undefined : settings.phaseConfig,
   245→    selected_phase_preset_id: settings.selectedPhasePresetId,
   246→    loras: settings.loras.map(l => ({ path: l.path, strength: l.strength })),
   247→    make_primary_variant: settings.makePrimaryVariant,
   248→    // Resolution
   249→    ...(context.projectResolution && { parsed_resolution_wh: context.projectResolution }),
   250→    // Structure video (from shot timeline data)
   251→    ...(structureVideos && { structure_videos: structureVideos }),
   252→  };
   253→}
   254→

   128→// ==================== Fetch Task ====================
   129→
   130→export const fetchTask = async (taskId: string): Promise<TaskData | null> => {
   131→  
   132→  try {
   133→    const { data, error } = await supabase
   134→      .from('tasks')
   135→      .select('*')
   136→      .eq('id', taskId)
   137→      .single();
   138→    
   139→    if (error || !data) {
   140→      console.error('[ApplySettings] ❌ Failed to fetch task:', error);
   141→      return null;
   142→    }
   143→    
   144→    const params = (data.params || {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
   145→    const orchestrator = (params.orchestrator_details || params.full_orchestrator_payload || {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
   146→    
   147→    return { params, orchestrator };
   148→  } catch (queryError) {
   149→    console.error('[ApplySettings] ❌ Exception fetching task:', queryError);
   150→    return null;
   151→  }
   152→};
   153→
   154→// ==================== Extract Settings ====================
   155→
   156→export const extractSettings = (taskData: TaskData): ExtractedSettings => {
   157→  // Cast to permissive record for dynamic property access from Supabase JSON
   158→  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
   159→  const params = taskData.params as Record<string, any>;
   160→  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
   161→  const orchestrator = taskData.orchestrator as Record<string, any>;
   162→  
   163→  // Extract all settings with fallbacks
   164→  const extracted: ExtractedSettings = {
   165→    // Prompts
   166→    prompt: (
   167→      (orchestrator.base_prompts_expanded?.[0] && orchestrator.base_prompts_expanded[0].trim()) ||
   168→      (orchestrator.base_prompt && orchestrator.base_prompt.trim()) ||
   169→      (params.prompt && params.prompt.trim()) ||
   170→      undefined
   171→    ),
   172→    prompts: orchestrator.base_prompts_expanded,
   173→    negativePrompt: orchestrator.negative_prompts_expanded?.[0] ?? params.negative_prompt,
   174→    negativePrompts: orchestrator.negative_prompts_expanded,
   175→    
   176→    // Generation settings
   177→    steps: orchestrator.steps ?? params.num_inference_steps,
   178→    frames: orchestrator.segment_frames_expanded?.[0] ?? params.segment_frames_expanded, // Legacy: single value for backward compat
   179→    segmentFramesExpanded: orchestrator.segment_frames_expanded ?? params.segment_frames_expanded, // NEW: full array of gaps
   180→    context: orchestrator.frame_overlap_expanded?.[0] ?? params.frame_overlap_expanded,
   181→    model: params.model_name ?? orchestrator.model_name,
   182→    
   183→    // Modes
   184→    generationMode: orchestrator.generation_mode ?? params.generation_mode,
   185→    // model_type is the existing field that stores I2V vs VACE mode
   186→    generationTypeMode: orchestrator.model_type ?? params.model_type,
   187→    advancedMode: orchestrator.advanced_mode ?? params.advanced_mode,
   188→    motionMode: orchestrator.motion_mode ?? params.motion_mode,
   189→    
   190→    // Advanced mode settings
   191→    phaseConfig: orchestrator.phase_config ?? params.phase_config,
   192→    selectedPhasePresetId: orchestrator.selected_phase_preset_id ?? params.selected_phase_preset_id,
   193→    turboMode: orchestrator.turbo_mode ?? params.turbo_mode,
   194→    enhancePrompt: orchestrator.enhance_prompt ?? params.enhance_prompt,
   195→    
   196→    // Text addons
   197→    textBeforePrompts: orchestrator.text_before_prompts ?? params.text_before_prompts,
   198→    textAfterPrompts: orchestrator.text_after_prompts ?? params.text_after_prompts,
   199→    
   200→    // Motion
   201→    amountOfMotion: orchestrator.amount_of_motion ?? params.amount_of_motion,
   202→    
   203→    // LoRAs - convert from object format to array format
   204→    // Backend stores as { "url": strength, ... } but we need [{ path, strength }, ...]
   205→    loras: (() => {
   206→      const loraData = orchestrator.loras ?? orchestrator.additional_loras ?? params.loras ?? params.additional_loras;
   207→      if (!loraData) return undefined;
   208→      
   209→      // If already array format, return as-is
   210→      if (Array.isArray(loraData)) return loraData;
   211→      
   212→      // Convert object format to array format
   213→      return Object.entries(loraData).map(([path, strength]) => ({
   214→        path,
   215→        strength: strength as number
   216→      }));
   217→    })(),
   218→    
   219→    // Structure video
   220→    structureVideoPath: orchestrator.structure_video_path ?? params.structure_video_path,
   221→    structureVideoTreatment: orchestrator.structure_video_treatment ?? params.structure_video_treatment,
   222→    structureVideoMotionStrength: orchestrator.structure_video_motion_strength ?? params.structure_video_motion_strength,
   223→    // Note: Backend uses both "structure_type" and "structure_video_type" - check both
   224→    structureVideoType: orchestrator.structure_video_type ?? orchestrator.structure_type ?? params.structure_video_type ?? params.structure_type,
   225→    
   226→    // Input images - extract from multiple possible locations
   227→    inputImages: (() => {
   228→      const cleanUrl = (url: string): string => {
   229→        if (typeof url !== 'string') return url;
   230→        return url.replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '');
   231→      };
   232→      
   233→      if (Array.isArray(params.input_images) && params.input_images.length > 0) {
   234→        return params.input_images.map(cleanUrl);
   235→      }
   236→      if (orchestrator.input_image_paths_resolved && Array.isArray(orchestrator.input_image_paths_resolved)) {
   237→        return orchestrator.input_image_paths_resolved.map(cleanUrl);
   238→      }
   239→      if (Array.isArray(params.input_image_paths_resolved)) {
   240→        return params.input_image_paths_resolved.map(cleanUrl);
   241→      }
   242→      return undefined;
   243→    })(),
   244→  };
   245→  
   246→  return extracted;
   247→};
   248→
   249→// ==================== Apply Functions ====================
   250→
   251→export const applyModelSettings = async (
   252→  settings: ExtractedSettings,
   253→  context: ApplyContext
   254→): Promise<ApplyResult> => {
   255→  if (!settings.model || settings.model === context.steerableMotionSettings.model_name) {
   256→    return { success: true, settingName: 'model', details: 'skipped - no change' };
   257→  }
   258→  
   259→  context.onSteerableMotionSettingsChange({ model_name: settings.model });
   260→  
   261→  return { success: true, settingName: 'model', details: settings.model };
   262→};
   263→
   264→export const applyPromptSettings = async (
   265→  settings: ExtractedSettings,
   266→  context: ApplyContext
   267→): Promise<ApplyResult> => {
   268→  // Apply main prompt
   269→  if (typeof settings.prompt === 'string' && settings.prompt.trim()) {
   270→    context.onBatchVideoPromptChange(settings.prompt);
   271→  }
   272→  
   273→  // Apply individual prompts to pair configs (regardless of current mode)
   274→  if (settings.prompts && settings.prompts.length > 1 && context.updatePairPromptsByIndex) {
   275→    
   276→    const errors: string[] = [];
   277→    
   278→    for (let i = 0; i < settings.prompts.length; i++) {
   279→      const pairPrompt = settings.prompts[i]?.trim();
   280→      const pairNegativePrompt = settings.negativePrompts?.[i]?.trim() || '';
   281→      
   282→      if (pairPrompt) {
   283→        try {
   284→          await context.updatePairPromptsByIndex(i, pairPrompt, pairNegativePrompt);
   285→        } catch (e) {
   286→          console.error(`[ApplySettings] ❌ Failed to apply prompt for pair ${i}:`, e);
   287→          errors.push(`Pair ${i}: ${e}`);
   288→        }
   289→      }
   290→    }
   291→    
   292→    if (errors.length > 0) {
   293→      return { success: false, settingName: 'prompts', error: errors.join('; ') };
   294→    }
   295→  }
   296→  
   297→  // Apply negative prompt
   298→  if (settings.negativePrompt !== undefined) {
   299→    context.onSteerableMotionSettingsChange({ negative_prompt: settings.negativePrompt || '' });
   300→  }
   301→  
   302→  return { success: true, settingName: 'prompts' };
   303→};
   304→
   305→export const applyGenerationSettings = async (
   306→  settings: ExtractedSettings,
   307→  context: ApplyContext
   308→): Promise<ApplyResult> => {
   309→  // Apply frames
   310→  if (typeof settings.frames === 'number' && !Number.isNaN(settings.frames)) {
   311→    context.onBatchVideoFramesChange(settings.frames);
   312→  }
   313→  
   314→  // Apply context
   315→  // Context frames removed - now fixed at 10 frames
   316→  
   317→  // Apply steps
   318→  if (typeof settings.steps === 'number' && !Number.isNaN(settings.steps)) {
   319→    context.onBatchVideoStepsChange(settings.steps);
   320→  }
   321→  
   322→  return { success: true, settingName: 'generation' };
   323→};
   324→
   325→export const applyModeSettings = async (
   326→  settings: ExtractedSettings,
   327→  context: ApplyContext
   328→): Promise<ApplyResult> => {
   329→  // Apply generation mode
   330→  if (settings.generationMode && (settings.generationMode === 'batch' || settings.generationMode === 'timeline' || settings.generationMode === 'by-pair')) {
   331→    context.onGenerationModeChange(settings.generationMode);
   332→  }
   333→  
   334→  // Apply advanced mode
   335→  if (settings.advancedMode !== undefined) {
   336→    context.onAdvancedModeChange(settings.advancedMode);
   337→  }
   338→  
   339→  // Apply motion mode
   340→  if (settings.motionMode !== undefined && context.onMotionModeChange) {
   341→    context.onMotionModeChange(settings.motionMode);
   342→  }
   343→  
   344→  // Apply generation type mode (I2V vs VACE)
   345→  if (settings.generationTypeMode !== undefined && context.onGenerationTypeModeChange) {
   346→    context.onGenerationTypeModeChange(settings.generationTypeMode);
   347→  }
   348→  
   349→  return { success: true, settingName: 'modes' };
   350→};
   351→
   352→export const applyAdvancedModeSettings = async (
   353→  settings: ExtractedSettings,
   354→  context: ApplyContext
   355→): Promise<ApplyResult> => {
   356→  // Helper to deep clone phase config to prevent shared references
   357→  const deepClonePhaseConfig = (config: typeof settings.phaseConfig) => {
   358→    if (!config) return undefined;
   359→    return {
   360→      ...config,
   361→      steps_per_phase: [...config.steps_per_phase],
   362→      phases: config.phases.map(phase => ({
   363→        ...phase,
   364→        loras: phase.loras.map(lora => ({ ...lora }))
   365→      }))
   366→    };
   367→  };
   368→  
   369→  // Apply phase config
   370→  if (settings.phaseConfig) {
   371→    // DEEP CLONE: Prevent shared references when applying task settings
   372→    const clonedConfig = deepClonePhaseConfig(settings.phaseConfig)!;
   373→    context.onPhaseConfigChange(clonedConfig);
   374→  }
   375→  
   376→  // Apply phase preset ID
   377→  if (settings.selectedPhasePresetId !== undefined) {
   378→    
   379→    if (settings.selectedPhasePresetId && context.onPhasePresetSelect && settings.phaseConfig) {
   380→      // Note: onPhasePresetSelect also deep clones, but we pass cloned config anyway for safety
   381→      const clonedConfig = deepClonePhaseConfig(settings.phaseConfig)!;
   382→      context.onPhasePresetSelect(settings.selectedPhasePresetId, clonedConfig);
   383→    } else if (!settings.selectedPhasePresetId && context.onPhasePresetRemove) {
   384→      context.onPhasePresetRemove();
   385→    }
   386→  }
   387→  
   388→  // Apply turbo mode
   389→  if (settings.turboMode !== undefined && context.onTurboModeChange) {
   390→    context.onTurboModeChange(settings.turboMode);
   391→  }
   392→  
   393→  // Apply enhance prompt
   394→  if (settings.enhancePrompt !== undefined && context.onEnhancePromptChange) {
   395→    context.onEnhancePromptChange(settings.enhancePrompt);
   396→  }
   397→  
   398→  return { success: true, settingName: 'advancedMode' };
   399→};
   400→

   402→  settings: ExtractedSettings,
   403→  context: ApplyContext
   404→): Promise<ApplyResult> => {
   405→  // Apply text before prompts
   406→  if (settings.textBeforePrompts !== undefined && context.onTextBeforePromptsChange) {
   407→    context.onTextBeforePromptsChange(settings.textBeforePrompts);
   408→  }
   409→  
   410→  // Apply text after prompts
   411→  if (settings.textAfterPrompts !== undefined && context.onTextAfterPromptsChange) {
   412→    context.onTextAfterPromptsChange(settings.textAfterPrompts);
   413→  }
   414→  
   415→  return { success: true, settingName: 'textAddons' };

   417→
   418→export const applyMotionSettings = async (
   419→  settings: ExtractedSettings,
   420→  context: ApplyContext
   421→): Promise<ApplyResult> => {
   422→  // Only apply if NOT in advanced mode
   423→  if (settings.amountOfMotion !== undefined && !settings.advancedMode && context.onAmountOfMotionChange) {
   424→    context.onAmountOfMotionChange(settings.amountOfMotion * 100);
   425→  } else if (settings.advancedMode) {
   426→  }
   427→  
   428→  return { success: true, settingName: 'motion' };
