```
   430→
   431→export const applyLoRAs = async (
   432→  settings: ExtractedSettings,
   433→  context: ApplyContext
   434→): Promise<ApplyResult> => {
   435→  // Only apply if NOT in advanced mode
   436→  if (settings.loras === undefined || settings.advancedMode) {
   437→    return { success: true, settingName: 'loras', details: 'skipped' };
   438→  }
   439→  
   440→  if (settings.loras && settings.loras.length > 0) {
   441→    
   442→    // Clear existing LoRAs first
   443→    if (context.loraManager.setSelectedLoras) {
   444→      context.loraManager.setSelectedLoras([]);
   445→    }
   446→    
   447→    // Map paths to available LoRAs and restore them (with delay to ensure state is cleared)
   448→    return new Promise((resolve) => {
   449→      setTimeout(() => {
   450→        let matchedCount = 0;
   451→        
   452→        settings.loras.forEach(loraData => {
   453→          const matchingLora = context.availableLoras.find(lora => {
   454→            const loraUrl = lora.huggingface_url || (lora as Record<string, unknown>)['Download Link'] || '';
   455→            return loraUrl === loraData.path ||
   456→                   loraUrl.endsWith(loraData.path) ||
   457→                   loraData.path.endsWith(loraUrl.split('/').pop() || '');
   458→          });
   459→          
   460→          if (matchingLora) {
   461→            context.loraManager.handleAddLora(matchingLora, false, loraData.strength);
   462→            matchedCount++;
   463→          }
   464→        });
   465→        
   466→        resolve({ success: true, settingName: 'loras', details: `${matchedCount}/${settings.loras.length} matched` });
   467→      }, 100); // Small delay to ensure state clears
   468→    });
   469→  } else {
   470→    if (context.loraManager.setSelectedLoras) {
   471→      context.loraManager.setSelectedLoras([]);
   472→    }
   473→    return { success: true, settingName: 'loras', details: 'cleared' };
   474→  }
   475→};
   476→
   477→// ==================== Apply Structure Video ====================
   478→
   479→export const applyStructureVideo = async (
   480→  settings: ExtractedSettings,
   481→  context: ApplyContext,
   482→  taskData: TaskData
   483→): Promise<ApplyResult> => {
   484→  const hasStructureVideoInTask = 'structure_video_path' in taskData.orchestrator || 'structure_video_path' in taskData.params;
   485→  
   486→  if (!hasStructureVideoInTask) {
   487→    return { success: true, settingName: 'structureVideo', details: 'skipped - not in task' };
   488→  }
   489→  
   490→  if (settings.structureVideoPath) {
   491→    
   492→    if (!context.handleStructureVideoChange) {
   493→      console.error('[ApplySettings] ❌ handleStructureVideoChange is not defined!');
   494→      return { success: false, settingName: 'structureVideo', error: 'handler not defined' };
   495→    }
   496→    
   497→    try {
   498→      let metadata = null;
   499→      try {
   500→        metadata = await extractVideoMetadataFromUrl(settings.structureVideoPath);
   501→      } catch (metadataError) {
   502→      }
   503→      
   504→      context.handleStructureVideoChange(
   505→        settings.structureVideoPath,
   506→        metadata,
   507→        settings.structureVideoTreatment || 'adjust',
   508→        settings.structureVideoMotionStrength ?? 1.0,
   509→        settings.structureVideoType || 'flow'
   510→      );
   511→      
   512→      return { success: true, settingName: 'structureVideo' };
   513→    } catch (error) {
   514→      const errorMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
   515→      console.error('[ApplySettings] ❌ Error applying structure video:', error);
   516→      return { success: false, settingName: 'structureVideo', error: errorMessage };
   517→    }
   518→  } else {
   519→    if (context.handleStructureVideoChange) {
   520→      context.handleStructureVideoChange(null, null, 'adjust', 1.0, 'flow');
   521→    }
   522→    return { success: true, settingName: 'structureVideo', details: 'cleared' };
   523→  }
   524→};
   525→
   526→// ==================== Apply Frame Positions ====================
   527→
   528→/**
   529→ * Apply frame positions from segment_frames_expanded to existing images
   530→ * This is used when settings are applied WITHOUT replacing images
   531→ */
   532→export const applyFramePositionsToExistingImages = async (
   533→  settings: ExtractedSettings,
   534→  selectedShot: Shot | null,
   535→  simpleFilteredImages: GenerationRow[]
   536→): Promise<ApplyResult> => {
   537→  const segmentGaps = settings.segmentFramesExpanded;
   538→  const hasSegmentGaps = Array.isArray(segmentGaps) && segmentGaps.length > 0;
   539→  
   540→  if (!hasSegmentGaps) {
   541→    return { success: true, settingName: 'framePositions', details: 'no data' };
   542→  }
   543→  
   544→  if (!selectedShot?.id) {
   545→    return { success: true, settingName: 'framePositions', details: 'skipped - no shot' };
   546→  }
   547→  
   548→  // Calculate cumulative positions from gaps
   549→  const cumulativePositions: number[] = [0]; // First image always at frame 0
   550→  for (let i = 0; i < segmentGaps.length; i++) {
   551→    const prevPosition = cumulativePositions[cumulativePositions.length - 1];
   552→    cumulativePositions.push(prevPosition + segmentGaps[i]);
   553→  }
   554→  
   555→  try {
   556→    // Update timeline_frame for each image
   557→    const updates = simpleFilteredImages.map(async (img, index) => {
   558→      // img.id is the shot_generations.id
   559→      if (!img.id) {
   560→        return null;
   561→      }
   562→      
   563→      // Use cumulative position if available
   564→      const newTimelineFrame = index < cumulativePositions.length 
   565→        ? cumulativePositions[index]
   566→        : cumulativePositions[cumulativePositions.length - 1] + (index - cumulativePositions.length + 1) * (segmentGaps[segmentGaps.length - 1] || 60);
   567→      
   568→      const { error } = await supabase
   569→        .from('shot_generations')
   570→        .update({ timeline_frame: newTimelineFrame })
   571→        .eq('id', img.id); // img.id is shot_generations.id
   572→      
   573→      if (error) {
   574→        console.error('[ApplySettings] ❌ Failed to update timeline_frame:', error);
   575→        return null;
   576→      }
   577→      
   578→      return { id: img.id, newTimelineFrame };
   579→    });
   580→    
   581→    const results = await Promise.all(updates);
   582→    const successCount = results.filter(r => r !== null).length;
   583→    
   584→    return {
   585→      success: true,
   586→      settingName: 'framePositions',
   587→      details: { updated: successCount, total: simpleFilteredImages.length }
   588→    };
   589→  } catch (e) {
   590→    const errorMessage = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
   591→    console.error('[ApplySettings] ❌ Error applying frame positions:', e);
   592→    return {
   593→      success: false,
   594→      settingName: 'framePositions',
   595→      error: errorMessage
   596→    };
   597→  }
   598→};
   599→
   600→// ==================== Replace Images ====================
   601→
   602→export const replaceImagesIfRequested = async (
   603→  settings: ExtractedSettings,
   604→  replaceImages: boolean,
   605→  inputImages: string[],
   606→  selectedShot: Shot | null,
   607→  projectId: string,
   608→  simpleFilteredImages: GenerationRow[],
   609→  addImageToShotMutation: { mutateAsync: (params: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<unknown> },
   610→  removeImageFromShotMutation: { mutateAsync: (params: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<unknown> }
   611→): Promise<ApplyResult> => {
   612→  if (!replaceImages) {
   613→    // NEW: Apply frame positions to existing images even when not replacing
   614→    return await applyFramePositionsToExistingImages(settings, selectedShot, simpleFilteredImages);
   615→  }
   616→  
   617→  if (!selectedShot?.id || !projectId) {
   618→    return { success: true, settingName: 'images', details: 'skipped - missing context' };
   619→  }
   620→  
   621→  // Use inputImages from params if passed array is empty but settings has them
   622→  const effectiveInputImages = (inputImages && inputImages.length > 0) 
   623→    ? inputImages 
   624→    : (settings.inputImages || []);
   625→  
   626→  if (effectiveInputImages.length === 0) {
   627→    return { success: false, settingName: 'images', error: 'No input images available' };
   628→  }
   629→  
   630→  try {
   631→    // Remove existing non-video images (only those with id)
   632→    const imagesToDelete = simpleFilteredImages.filter((img): img is GenerationRow & { id: string } => !!img.id);
   633→
   634→    const deletions = imagesToDelete.map(img => removeImageFromShotMutation.mutateAsync({
   635→      shotId: selectedShot.id,
   636→      shotGenerationId: img.id, // img.id is shot_generations.id - narrowed by filter above
   637→      projectId: projectId,
   638→    }));
   639→    
   640→    if (deletions.length > 0) {
   641→      await Promise.allSettled(deletions);
   642→    }
   643→    
   644→    // Calculate timeline positions from segment_frames_expanded array
   645→    // segment_frames_expanded contains GAPS between successive frames
   646→    // e.g., [65, 37, 21] means: image0=0, image1=0+65=65, image2=65+37=102, image3=102+21=123
   647→    const segmentGaps = settings.segmentFramesExpanded;
   648→    const hasSegmentGaps = Array.isArray(segmentGaps) && segmentGaps.length > 0;
   649→    
   650→    // Calculate cumulative positions from gaps
   651→    let cumulativePositions: number[] = [];
   652→    if (hasSegmentGaps) {
   653→      cumulativePositions = [0]; // First image always at frame 0
   654→      for (let i = 0; i < segmentGaps.length; i++) {
   655→        const prevPosition = cumulativePositions[cumulativePositions.length - 1];
   656→        cumulativePositions.push(prevPosition + segmentGaps[i]);
   657→      }
   658→    }
   659→    
   660→    // Fallback to uniform spacing if no segment_frames_expanded
   661→    const uniformSpacing = settings.frames || 60;
   662→    
   663→    // Look up generation IDs for all input image URLs
   664→    const { data: generationLookup, error: lookupError } = await supabase
   665→      .from('generations')
   666→      .select('id, location, thumbnail_url')
   667→      .in('location', effectiveInputImages);
   668→    
   669→    if (lookupError) {
   670→      console.error('[ApplySettings] ❌ Failed to look up generations by URL:', lookupError);
   671→      return { success: false, settingName: 'images', error: 'Failed to look up generation IDs for input images' };
   672→    }
   673→    
   674→    // Create a map of URL -> generation data for quick lookup
   675→    const urlToGeneration = new Map<string, { id: string; location: string; thumbnail_url: string | null }>();
   676→    (generationLookup || []).forEach(gen => {
   677→      if (gen.location) {
   678→        urlToGeneration.set(gen.location, gen);
   679→      }
   680→    });
   681→    
   682→    // Add input images in order with calculated timeline_frame positions
   683→    const additions = effectiveInputImages.map((url: string, index: number) => {
   684→      // Use cumulative position if available, otherwise fall back to uniform spacing
   685→      const timelineFrame = hasSegmentGaps && index < cumulativePositions.length
   686→        ? cumulativePositions[index]
   687→        : index * uniformSpacing;
   688→      
   689→      // Look up the generation for this URL
   690→      const generation = urlToGeneration.get(url);
   691→      
   692→      if (!generation) {
   693→        return Promise.resolve(); // Skip this image
   694→      }
   695→      
   696→      return addImageToShotMutation.mutateAsync({
   697→        shot_id: selectedShot.id,
   698→        generation_id: generation.id,
   699→        project_id: projectId,
   700→        imageUrl: url,
   701→        thumbUrl: generation.thumbnail_url || url,
   702→        timelineFrame: timelineFrame,
   703→      });
   704→    });
   705→    
   706→    if (additions.length > 0) {
   707→      await Promise.allSettled(additions);
   708→    }
   709→    
   710→    return {
   711→      success: true,
   712→      settingName: 'images',
   713→      details: { removed: imagesToDelete.length, added: effectiveInputImages.length }
   714→    };
   715→  } catch (e) {
   716→    const errorMessage = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
   717→    console.error('[ApplySettings] ❌ Error replacing images:', e);
   718→    return {
   719→      success: false,
   720→      settingName: 'images',
   721→      error: errorMessage
   722→    };
   723→  }
   724→};
   725→
   726→
```

```
    12→SRC_PATH = PROJECT_ROOT / os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_SRC", "src")

    17→    "dist", "build", ".next", ".nuxt", ".output",

    27→def set_exclusions(patterns: list[str]):
    28→    """Set global exclusion patterns (called once from CLI at startup)."""

    35→
    36→_file_cache: dict[str, str | None] = {}
    37→_cache_enabled = False
    38→
    39→
    40→def enable_file_cache():
    41→    """Enable scan-scoped file content cache."""
    42→    global _cache_enabled
    43→    _cache_enabled = True
    44→    _file_cache.clear()
    45→
    46→
    47→def disable_file_cache():
    48→    """Disable file content cache and free memory."""

    50→    _cache_enabled = False
    51→    _file_cache.clear()
    52→
    53→
    54→# ── Atomic file writes ─────────────────────────────────────
    55→
    56→import tempfile
    57→
    58→
    59→def safe_write_text(filepath: str | Path, content: str) -> None:
    60→    """Atomically write text to a file using temp+rename."""
    61→    p = Path(filepath)
    62→    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    63→    fd, tmp = tempfile.mkstemp(dir=p.parent, suffix=".tmp")
    64→    try:
    65→        with os.fdopen(fd, "w") as f:
    66→            f.write(content)
    67→        os.replace(tmp, str(p))
    68→    except BaseException:
    69→        try:
    70→            os.unlink(tmp)
    71→        except OSError:
    72→            pass
    73→        raise
    74→
    75→
    76→# ── Cross-platform grep replacements ────────────────────────
    77→
    78→
    79→def _read_file_text(filepath: str) -> str | None:
    80→    """Read a file as text, with optional caching."""
    81→    if _cache_enabled:
    82→        if filepath in _file_cache:
    83→            return _file_cache[filepath]
    84→    try:
    85→        content = Path(filepath).read_text(errors="replace")
    86→    except OSError:
    87→        content = None
    88→    if _cache_enabled:
    89→        _file_cache[filepath] = content
    90→    return content
    91→
    92→
    93→def grep_files(pattern: str, file_list: list[str], *,
    94→               flags: int = 0) -> list[tuple[str, int, str]]:
    95→    """Search files for a regex pattern. Returns list of (filepath, lineno, line_text).
    96→
    97→    Cross-platform replacement for ``grep -rn -E <pattern> <path>``.
    98→    """
    99→    compiled = re.compile(pattern, flags)
   100→    results: list[tuple[str, int, str]] = []
   101→    for filepath in file_list:
   102→        abs_path = filepath if os.path.isabs(filepath) else str(PROJECT_ROOT / filepath)
   103→        content = _read_file_text(abs_path)
   104→        if content is None:
   105→            continue
   106→        for lineno, line in enumerate(content.splitlines(), 1):
   107→            if compiled.search(line):
   108→                results.append((filepath, lineno, line))
   109→    return results
   110→
   111→
   112→def grep_files_containing(names: set[str], file_list: list[str], *,
   113→                          word_boundary: bool = True) -> dict[str, set[str]]:
   114→    r"""Find which files contain which names. Returns {name: set(filepaths)}.
   115→
   116→    Cross-platform replacement for ``grep -rlFw -f patternfile <path>``
   117→    followed by per-file ``grep -oFw``.
   118→    """
   119→    if not names:
   120→        return {}
   121→    if word_boundary:
   122→        escaped = sorted(names, key=len, reverse=True)
   123→        combined = re.compile(r"\b(?:" + "|".join(re.escape(n) for n in escaped) + r")\b")
   124→    else:
   125→        escaped = sorted(names, key=len, reverse=True)
   126→        combined = re.compile("|".join(re.escape(n) for n in escaped))
   127→
   128→    name_to_files: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
   129→    for filepath in file_list:
   130→        abs_path = filepath if os.path.isabs(filepath) else str(PROJECT_ROOT / filepath)
   131→        content = _read_file_text(abs_path)
   132→        if content is None:
   133→            continue
   134→        found = set(combined.findall(content))
   135→        for name in found & names:
   136→            name_to_files.setdefault(name, set()).add(filepath)
   137→    return name_to_files
   138→
   139→
   140→def grep_count_files(name: str, file_list: list[str], *,
   141→                     word_boundary: bool = True) -> list[str]:
   142→    """Return list of files containing name. Replacement for ``grep -rl -w name``."""
   143→    if word_boundary:
   144→        pat = re.compile(r"\b" + re.escape(name) + r"\b")
   145→    else:
   146→        pat = re.compile(re.escape(name))
   147→    matching: list[str] = []
   148→    for filepath in file_list:
   149→        abs_path = filepath if os.path.isabs(filepath) else str(PROJECT_ROOT / filepath)
   150→        content = _read_file_text(abs_path)
   151→        if content is None:
   152→            continue
   153→        if pat.search(content):
   154→            matching.append(filepath)
   155→    return matching

   172→
   173→
   174→def c(text: str, color: str) -> str:
   175→    if NO_COLOR or not sys.stdout.isatty():
   176→        return str(text)
   177→    return f"{COLORS.get(color, '')}{text}{COLORS['reset']}"
   178→
   179→
   180→def log(msg: str):
   181→    """Print a dim status message to stderr."""
   182→    print(c(msg, "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
   183→
   184→
   185→def print_table(headers: list[str], rows: list[list[str]], widths: list[int] | None = None):
   186→    if not rows:
   187→        return
   188→    if not widths:
   189→        widths = [max(len(str(h)), *(len(str(r[i])) for r in rows)) for i, h in enumerate(headers)]
   190→    header_line = "  ".join(h.ljust(w) for h, w in zip(headers, widths))
   191→    print(c(header_line, "bold"))
   192→    print(c("─" * (sum(widths) + 2 * (len(widths) - 1)), "dim"))
   193→    for row in rows:
   194→        print("  ".join(str(v).ljust(w) for v, w in zip(row, widths)))
   195→
   196→
   197→def display_entries(args, entries, *, label, empty_msg, columns, widths, row_fn,
   198→                    json_payload=None, overflow=True):
   199→    """Standard JSON/empty/table display for detect commands.
   200→
   201→    Handles the three-branch pattern shared by most cmd wrappers:
   202→    1. --json → dump payload  2. empty → green message  3. table → header + rows + overflow.
   203→    Returns True if entries were displayed, False if empty.
   204→    """
   205→    import json as _json
   206→    if getattr(args, "json", False):
   207→        payload = json_payload or {"count": len(entries), "entries": entries}
   208→        print(_json.dumps(payload, indent=2))
   209→        return True
   210→    if not entries:
   211→        print(c(empty_msg, "green"))
   212→        return False
   213→    print(c(f"\n{label}: {len(entries)}\n", "bold"))
   214→    top = getattr(args, "top", 20)
   215→    rows = [row_fn(e) for e in entries[:top]]
   216→    print_table(columns, rows, widths)
   217→    if overflow and len(entries) > top:
   218→        print(f"\n  ... and {len(entries) - top} more")
   219→    return True
   220→
   221→
   222→def rel(path: str) -> str:
   223→    try:
   224→        return str(Path(path).resolve().relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT)).replace("\\", "/")
   225→    except ValueError:
   226→        return path
   227→
   228→
   229→def resolve_path(filepath: str) -> str:
   230→    """Resolve a filepath to absolute, handling both relative and absolute."""
   231→    p = Path(filepath)
   232→    if p.is_absolute():
   233→        return str(p.resolve())
   234→    return str((PROJECT_ROOT / filepath).resolve())
   235→
   236→
   237→def matches_exclusion(rel_path: str, exclusion: str) -> bool:
   238→    """Check if a relative path matches an exclusion pattern (path-component aware).
   239→
   240→    Matches if exclusion is a path component (e.g. "test" matches "test/foo.py"
   241→    or "src/test/bar.py") or a directory prefix (e.g. "src/test" matches
   242→    "src/test/bar.py"). Does NOT do substring matching — "test" will NOT match
   243→    "testimony.py".
   244→    """
   245→    parts = Path(rel_path).parts
   246→    # Direct component match
   247→    if exclusion in parts:
   248→        return True
   249→    # Directory prefix match (e.g. "src/test" matches "src/test/bar.py")
   250→    if "/" in exclusion or os.sep in exclusion:
   251→        normalized = exclusion.rstrip("/").rstrip(os.sep)
   252→        return rel_path.startswith(normalized + "/") or rel_path.startswith(normalized + os.sep)
   253→    return False
   254→
   255→
   256→def _is_excluded_dir(name: str, rel_path: str, extra: tuple[str, ...]) -> bool:
   257→    """Check if a directory should be pruned during traversal."""
   258→    if name in DEFAULT_EXCLUSIONS or name.endswith(".egg-info"):
   259→        return True
   260→    if extra and any(matches_exclusion(rel_path, ex) or ex == name for ex in extra):
   261→        return True
   262→    return False
   263→
   264→
   265→@lru_cache(maxsize=16)
   266→def _find_source_files_cached(path: str, extensions: tuple[str, ...],
   267→                               exclusions: tuple[str, ...] | None = None,
   268→                               extra_exclusions: tuple[str, ...] = ()) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   269→    """Cached file discovery using os.walk — cross-platform, prunes during traversal."""
   270→    root = Path(path)
   271→    if not root.is_absolute():
   272→        root = PROJECT_ROOT / root
   273→    all_exclusions = (exclusions or ()) + extra_exclusions
   274→    ext_set = set(extensions)
   275→    files: list[str] = []
   276→    for dirpath, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(root):
   277→        # Prune excluded directories in-place (prevents descending into them)
   278→        rel_dir = os.path.relpath(dirpath, PROJECT_ROOT).replace("\\", "/")
   279→        dirnames[:] = sorted(
   280→            d for d in dirnames
   281→            if not _is_excluded_dir(d, rel_dir + "/" + d, all_exclusions)
   282→        )
   283→        for fname in filenames:
   284→            if any(fname.endswith(ext) for ext in ext_set):
   285→                full = os.path.join(dirpath, fname)
   286→                rel_file = os.path.relpath(full, PROJECT_ROOT).replace("\\", "/")
   287→                if all_exclusions and any(matches_exclusion(rel_file, ex) for ex in all_exclusions):
   288→                    continue
   289→                files.append(rel_file)
   290→    return tuple(sorted(files))
   291→
   292→
   293→def find_source_files(path: str | Path, extensions: list[str],
   294→                      exclusions: list[str] | None = None) -> list[str]:
   295→    """Find all files with given extensions under a path, excluding patterns."""
   296→    # Pass _extra_exclusions as part of the cache key so changes invalidate cached results
   297→    return list(_find_source_files_cached(
   298→        str(path), tuple(extensions), tuple(exclusions) if exclusions else None,
   299→        _extra_exclusions))
   300→
   301→
   302→def find_ts_files(path: str | Path) -> list[str]:
   303→    """Find all .ts and .tsx files under a path."""
   304→    return find_source_files(path, [".ts", ".tsx"])
   305→
   306→
   307→def find_tsx_files(path: str | Path) -> list[str]:
   308→    """Find all .tsx files under a path."""
   309→    return find_source_files(path, [".tsx"])
   310→
   311→
   312→def find_py_files(path: str | Path) -> list[str]:
   313→    """Find all .py files under a path."""
   314→    return find_source_files(path, [".py"])
   315→
   316→
   317→TOOL_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
   318→
   319→
   320→def compute_tool_hash() -> str:
   321→    """Compute a content hash of all .py files in the desloppify package.
   322→
   323→    Changes to any tool source file produce a different hash, enabling
   324→    staleness detection for scan results.
   325→    """
   326→    h = hashlib.sha256()
   327→    for py_file in sorted(TOOL_DIR.rglob("*.py")):
   328→        try:
   329→            h.update(str(py_file.relative_to(TOOL_DIR)).encode())
   330→            h.update(py_file.read_bytes())
   331→        except OSError:
   332→            continue
   333→    return h.hexdigest()[:12]
   334→
   335→
   336→def check_tool_staleness(state: dict) -> str | None:
   337→    """Return a warning string if tool code has changed since last scan, else None."""
   338→    stored = state.get("tool_hash")
   339→    if not stored:
   340→        return None
   341→    current = compute_tool_hash()
   342→    if current != stored:
   343→        return (f"Tool code changed since last scan (was {stored}, now {current}). "
   344→                f"Consider re-running: desloppify scan")
   345→    return None
   346→
   347→
   348→def get_area(filepath: str) -> str:
   349→    """Derive an area name from a file path (generic: first 2 path components)."""
   350→    parts = filepath.split("/")
   351→    return "/".join(parts[:2]) if len(parts) > 1 else parts[0]
   352→
```

```
     2→import { TASK_NAME_ABBREVIATIONS } from '../constants';
     3→import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
     4→
     5→// TODO: type properly — task params are deeply nested JSON with varying shapes per task type
     6→// Using index signature to allow deep property access without full structural typing
     7→type DeepJsonParams = { [key: string]: DeepJsonParams | string | string[] | number | boolean | null | undefined };
     8→
     9→/**
    10→ * Derive input images from task params
    11→ * Extracts image URLs from various param locations based on task type
    12→ */
    13→export const deriveInputImages = (task: Task | null): string[] => {
    14→  if (!task?.params) return [];
    15→  const params = task.params as DeepJsonParams;
    16→
    17→  // For individual_travel_segment, use top-level or individual_segment_params (2 images only)
    18→  if (task.taskType === 'individual_travel_segment') {
    19→    const images = (params.individual_segment_params as DeepJsonParams)?.input_image_paths_resolved ||
    20→                   params.input_image_paths_resolved ||
    21→                   [];
    22→    return (Array.isArray(images) ? images : []).filter(Boolean) as string[];
    23→  }
    24→
    25→  const inputImages: string[] = [];
    26→  if (typeof params.input_image === 'string') inputImages.push(params.input_image);
    27→  if (typeof params.image === 'string') inputImages.push(params.image);
    28→  if (typeof params.init_image === 'string') inputImages.push(params.init_image);
    29→  if (typeof params.control_image === 'string') inputImages.push(params.control_image);
    30→  if (params.images && Array.isArray(params.images)) inputImages.push(...params.images as string[]);
    31→  if (params.input_images && Array.isArray(params.input_images)) inputImages.push(...params.input_images as string[]);
    32→  // For travel tasks, also check orchestrator paths
    33→  const orchestratorPayload = params.full_orchestrator_payload as DeepJsonParams | undefined;
    34→  if (orchestratorPayload?.input_image_paths_resolved && Array.isArray(orchestratorPayload.input_image_paths_resolved)) {
    35→    inputImages.push(...orchestratorPayload.input_image_paths_resolved as string[]);
    36→  }
    37→  const orchestratorDetails = params.orchestrator_details as DeepJsonParams | undefined;
    38→  if (orchestratorDetails?.input_image_paths_resolved && Array.isArray(orchestratorDetails.input_image_paths_resolved)) {
    39→    inputImages.push(...orchestratorDetails.input_image_paths_resolved as string[]);
    40→  }
    41→  // Also check top-level input_image_paths_resolved
    42→  if (params.input_image_paths_resolved && Array.isArray(params.input_image_paths_resolved)) {
    43→    inputImages.push(...params.input_image_paths_resolved as string[]);
    44→  }
    45→  return inputImages.filter(Boolean);
    46→};

   101→ * Extract parent generation ID from task params
   102→ * For edit-video tasks, the REAL parent is in task params, not the generation's parent_generation_id
   103→ */
   104→export const extractTaskParentGenerationId = (params: Record<string, unknown>): string | undefined => {
   105→  const p = params as DeepJsonParams;
   106→  return (
   107→    (p?.parent_generation_id as string) ||
   108→    ((p?.orchestrator_details as DeepJsonParams)?.parent_generation_id as string) ||
   109→    ((p?.full_orchestrator_payload as DeepJsonParams)?.parent_generation_id as string) ||
   110→    undefined
   111→  );

   113→
   114→/**
   115→ * Extract pair_shot_generation_id from task params for segment videos.
   116→ * This identifies which timeline pair the segment belongs to.
   117→ */
   118→export const extractPairShotGenerationId = (task: Task): string | null => {
   119→  const params = task.params as DeepJsonParams;
   120→
   121→  const orchDetails = params?.orchestrator_details as DeepJsonParams | undefined;
   122→  const pairIds = orchDetails?.pair_shot_generation_ids as string[] | undefined;
   123→  const segmentIndex = (params?.segment_index as number) ?? 0;
   124→
   125→  return (
   126→    (params?.pair_shot_generation_id as string) ||
   127→    ((params?.individual_segment_params as DeepJsonParams)?.pair_shot_generation_id as string) ||
   128→    (pairIds?.[segmentIndex]) ||
   129→    null
   130→  );
   131→};
   132→
   133→/**
   134→ * Check if a task is a segment video (individual_travel_segment)
   135→ * These should open in shot context for proper timeline integration.
   136→ */
   137→export const isSegmentVideoTask = (task: Task): boolean => {
   138→  return task.taskType === 'individual_travel_segment';
   139→};
   140→
   141→/**
   142→ * Check if a segment is still connected to its shot's timeline.
   143→ * Returns true if the pair_shot_generation_id exists in shot_generations
   144→ * for the given shot and is positioned on the timeline.
   145→ */
   146→export const checkSegmentConnection = async (
   147→  pairShotGenerationId: string | null,
   148→  shotId: string
   149→): Promise<boolean> => {
   150→  if (!pairShotGenerationId) return false;
   151→
   152→  const { data } = await supabase
   153→    .from('shot_generations')
   154→    .select('id, shot_id, timeline_frame')
   155→    .eq('id', pairShotGenerationId)
   156→    .maybeSingle();
   157→
   158→  // Check if it exists, belongs to the right shot, and is on timeline
   159→  return !!data && data.shot_id === shotId && (data.timeline_frame ?? -1) >= 0;
   160→};
```

```
1→import { quantizeGap } from "./time-utils";
     2→
     3→// The trailing endpoint key — lives in the positions map but is not a real image
     4→export const [REDACTED];
     5→
     6→// Phantom key for a pending item (duplicate/drop/external add) — used in augmented
     7→// positions maps so pair regions and segment strip can update before the real item arrives
     8→export const PENDING_POSITION_KEY = '__pending__';
     9→
    10→// Minimum gap between frames (4N+1 format, starting at 5)
    11→const QUANTIZE_MIN_GAP = 5;
    12→
    13→/**
    14→ * Quantize all positions to ensure gaps between adjacent items are in 4N+1 format.
    15→ * First item remains at position 0, subsequent items are adjusted to have quantized gaps.
    16→ * The trailing endpoint is extracted before quantizing and re-added after (its gap has
    17→ * different constraints — quantization happens in the drag handler).
    18→ */
    19→export const quantizePositions = (positions: Map<string, number>): Map<string, number> => {
    20→  // Extract trailing endpoint before quantizing
    21→  const trailingValue = positions.get(TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY);
    22→  const imagePositions = new Map(positions);
    23→  imagePositions.delete(TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY);
    24→
    25→  const entries = [...imagePositions.entries()].sort((a, b) => a[1] - b[1]);
    26→
    27→  if (entries.length === 0) {
    28→    // If only trailing existed, return it as-is
    29→    const result = new Map<string, number>();
    30→    if (trailingValue !== undefined) result.set(TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY, trailingValue);
    31→    return result;
    32→  }
    33→
    34→  const result = new Map<string, number>();
    35→
    36→  // First item always at 0
    37→  result.set(entries[0][0], 0);
    38→
    39→  // Subsequent items: quantize the gap from previous
    40→  for (let i = 1; i < entries.length; i++) {
    41→    const [id, pos] = entries[i];
    42→    const prevPos = result.get(entries[i - 1][0]) ?? 0;
    43→    const originalGap = pos - entries[i - 1][1];
    44→    const quantizedGap = quantizeGap(Math.max(originalGap, QUANTIZE_MIN_GAP), QUANTIZE_MIN_GAP);
    45→    result.set(id, prevPos + quantizedGap);
    46→  }
    47→
    48→  // Re-add trailing endpoint unchanged
    49→  if (trailingValue !== undefined) {
    50→    result.set(TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY, trailingValue);
    51→  }
    52→
    53→  return result;
    54→};
    55→
    56→// Calculate max gap based on context frames (81 frames default)
    57→const calculateMaxGap = (contextFrames: number = 0, baseMax: number = 81): number => {
    58→  return Math.max(1, baseMax - (contextFrames * 2));
    59→};
    60→
    61→// Minimum gap between frames (4N+1 format, starting at 5)
    62→const MIN_GAP = 5;
    63→
    64→
    65→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    66→// Shrink oversized gaps and enforce frame 0 constraint.
    67→// First item is always placed at position 0.
    68→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    69→const shrinkOversizedGaps = (
    70→  positions: Map<string, number>,
    71→  excludeId?: string,
    72→  skipQuantization: boolean = false,
    73→): Map<string, number> => {
    74→  const maxGap = calculateMaxGap();
    75→
    76→  // Preserve trailing endpoint separately (not subject to gap shrinking)
    77→  const trailingValue = positions.get(TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY);
    78→
    79→  const entries = [...positions.entries()].filter(([id]) => id !== excludeId && id !== TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY);
    80→
    81→  // Ensure frame 0 is included if it exists
    82→  if (!entries.some(([_, pos]) => pos === 0)) {
    83→    const zeroId = [...positions.entries()].find(([_, pos]) => pos === 0)?.[0];
    84→    if (zeroId) entries.push([zeroId, 0]);
    85→  }
    86→
    87→  entries.sort((a, b) => a[1] - b[1]);
    88→
    89→  const result = new Map<string, number>();
    90→
    91→  // First item always at position 0 (this enforces the frame 0 constraint)
    92→  for (let i = 0; i < entries.length; i++) {
    93→    const [id, originalPos] = entries[i];
    94→
    95→    if (i === 0) {
    96→      result.set(id, 0);
    97→    } else {
    98→      const prevPos = result.get(entries[i - 1][0]) ?? 0;
    99→      const prevOriginalPos = entries[i - 1][1];
   100→
   101→      const originalGap = originalPos - prevOriginalPos;
   102→      const desiredGap = Math.max(originalGap, MIN_GAP);
   103→      const quantizedDesiredGap = skipQuantization ? desiredGap : quantizeGap(desiredGap, MIN_GAP);
   104→      const constrainedGap = Math.min(quantizedDesiredGap, maxGap);
   105→      const finalGap = skipQuantization ? constrainedGap : quantizeGap(constrainedGap, MIN_GAP);
   106→
   107→      result.set(id, prevPos + finalGap);
   108→    }
   109→  }
   110→
   111→  // Re-add excluded id unchanged
   112→  if (excludeId && positions.has(excludeId)) {
   113→    result.set(excludeId, positions.get(excludeId)!);
   114→  }
   115→
   116→  // Re-add trailing endpoint unchanged
   117→  if (trailingValue !== undefined) {
   118→    result.set(TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY, trailingValue);
   119→  }
   120→
   121→  return result;
   122→};
   123→
   124→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   125→// Apply fluid timeline behavior with movement limiting
   126→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   127→export const applyFluidTimeline = (
   128→  positions: Map<string, number>,
   129→  draggedId: string,
   130→  targetFrame: number,
   131→  excludeId?: string,
   132→  _fullMin: number = 0,
   133→  _fullMax: number = Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER,
   134→  skipQuantization: boolean = false,
   135→): Map<string, number> => {
   136→  const originalPos = positions.get(draggedId) ?? 0;
   137→  const movementAmount = targetFrame - originalPos;
   138→
   139→  // Limit movement to 50 frames per drag for natural feel
   140→  const maxSingleMove = 50;
   141→  const limitedMovement = Math.max(-maxSingleMove, Math.min(maxSingleMove, movementAmount));
   142→  const limitedTargetFrame = originalPos + limitedMovement;
   143→
   144→  const result = new Map(positions);
   145→  result.set(draggedId, limitedTargetFrame);
   146→
   147→  // Apply gap constraints (this also handles frame 0 enforcement)
   148→  return shrinkOversizedGaps(result, excludeId, skipQuantization);
   149→};
   150→
   151→// Convert pixel position to frame number
   152→export const pixelToFrame = (pixelX: number, containerWidth: number, fullMin: number, fullRange: number): number => {
   153→  const fraction = pixelX / containerWidth;
   154→  return Math.round(fullMin + fraction * fullRange);
   155→};
   156→
   157→// Calculate timeline dimensions
   158→const MINIMUM_TIMELINE_MAX = 30;
   159→
   160→export const getTimelineDimensions = (
   161→  framePositions: Map<string, number>,
   162→  pendingFrames?: (number | null)[]
   163→) => {
   164→  const positions = Array.from(framePositions.values());
   165→  const validPendingFrames = (pendingFrames || []).filter((f): f is number => f !== null && f !== undefined);
   166→  const allPositions = [...positions, ...validPendingFrames];
   167→
   168→  if (allPositions.length === 0) {
   169→    return { fullMin: 0, fullMax: MINIMUM_TIMELINE_MAX, fullRange: MINIMUM_TIMELINE_MAX };
   170→  }
   171→
   172→  const staticMax = Math.max(...allPositions, 0);
   173→  const staticMin = Math.min(...allPositions, 0);
   174→  const fullMax = Math.max(staticMax, MINIMUM_TIMELINE_MAX);
   175→  const fullMin = Math.min(0, staticMin);
   176→  const fullRange = Math.max(fullMax - fullMin, 1);
   177→
   178→  return { fullMin, fullMax, fullRange };
   179→};
   180→
   181→
   182→// Get pair information from positions (excludes trailing endpoint)
   183→export const getPairInfo = (framePositions: Map<string, number>) => {
   184→  const sortedPositions = [...framePositions.entries()]
   185→    .filter(([id]) => id !== TRAILING_ENDPOINT_KEY)
   186→    .map(([id, pos]) => ({ id, pos }))
   187→    .sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);
   188→
   189→  const pairs = [];
   190→  for (let i = 0; i < sortedPositions.length - 1; i++) {
   191→    const startFrame = sortedPositions[i].pos;
   192→    const endFrame = sortedPositions[i + 1].pos;
   193→    const pairFrames = endFrame - startFrame;
   194→
   195→    pairs.push({
   196→      index: i,
   197→      startFrame,
   198→      endFrame,
   199→      frames: pairFrames,
   200→      generationStart: startFrame,
   201→      contextStart: endFrame,
   202→      contextEnd: endFrame,
   203→    });
   204→  }
   205→
   206→  return pairs;
   207→};
   208→
   209→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   210→// Multi-select bundling: bundle selected items 5 frames apart
   211→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   212→const BUNDLE_GAP = 5;
   213→
   214→export const applyFluidTimelineMulti = (
   215→  positions: Map<string, number>,
   216→  selectedIds: string[],
   217→  targetFrame: number,
   218→  skipQuantization: boolean = false,
   219→): Map<string, number> => {
   220→  if (selectedIds.length === 0) return positions;
   221→  if (selectedIds.length === 1) {
   222→    return applyFluidTimeline(positions, selectedIds[0], targetFrame, undefined, 0, Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER, skipQuantization);
   223→  }
   224→
   225→  const result = new Map(positions);
   226→
   227→  // Sort selected items by current position to preserve relative order
   228→  const selectedWithPositions = selectedIds
   229→    .map(id => ({ id, frame: positions.get(id) ?? 0 }))
   230→    .sort((a, b) => a.frame - b.frame);
   231→
   232→  // Place selected items 5 frames apart
   233→  selectedWithPositions.forEach((item, index) => {
   234→    result.set(item.id, targetFrame + (index * BUNDLE_GAP));
   235→  });
   236→
   237→  // Push out non-selected items that are within the bundle range
   238→  const bundleEnd = targetFrame + ((selectedIds.length - 1) * BUNDLE_GAP);
   239→  const nonSelectedItems = [...positions.entries()]
   240→    .filter(([id]) => !selectedIds.includes(id));
   241→
   242→  nonSelectedItems.forEach(([id, pos]) => {
   243→    if (pos >= targetFrame && pos <= bundleEnd) {
   244→      result.set(id, bundleEnd + BUNDLE_GAP);
   245→    }
   246→  });
   247→
   248→  // Apply gap constraints (handles frame 0 enforcement)
   249→  return shrinkOversizedGaps(result, undefined, skipQuantization);
   250→};
   251→
   252→/**
   253→ * Calculate placement for a new structure video on the timeline.
   254→ * Places after the last existing video, clipping the last video if needed to make space.
   255→ *
   256→ * @returns Object with start_frame, end_frame, and optional lastVideoUpdate if clipping is needed
   257→ */
   258→interface VideoPlacementResult {
   259→  start_frame: number;
   260→  end_frame: number;
   261→  lastVideoUpdate?: { index: number; newEndFrame: number };
   262→}
   263→
   264→export const calculateNewVideoPlacement = (
   265→  videoFrameCount: number,
   266→  existingVideos: Array<{ path: string; start_frame: number; end_frame: number }> | undefined,
   267→  fullMax: number
   268→): VideoPlacementResult => {
   269→  let start_frame = 0;
   270→  let end_frame = videoFrameCount;
   271→  let lastVideoUpdate: { index: number; newEndFrame: number } | undefined;
   272→
   273→  if (existingVideos && existingVideos.length > 0) {
   274→    const sorted = [...existingVideos].sort((a, b) => a.start_frame - b.start_frame);
   275→    const lastVideo = sorted[sorted.length - 1];
   276→    const lastVideoIndex = existingVideos.findIndex(
   277→      v => v.path === lastVideo.path && v.start_frame === lastVideo.start_frame
   278→    );
   279→
   280→    start_frame = lastVideo.end_frame;
   281→    end_frame = start_frame + videoFrameCount;
   282→
   283→    // If no space on timeline, clip 1/5 of the last video's range
   284→    if (start_frame >= fullMax && lastVideoIndex >= 0) {
   285→      const lastVideoRange = lastVideo.end_frame - lastVideo.start_frame;
   286→      const clipAmount = Math.max(10, Math.floor(lastVideoRange / 5));
   287→      const newLastVideoEnd = lastVideo.end_frame - clipAmount;
   288→
   289→      if (newLastVideoEnd > lastVideo.start_frame + 10) {
   290→        lastVideoUpdate = { index: lastVideoIndex, newEndFrame: newLastVideoEnd };
   291→        start_frame = newLastVideoEnd;
   292→        end_frame = start_frame + videoFrameCount;
   293→      }
   294→    }
   295→  }
   296→
   297→  return { start_frame, end_frame, lastVideoUpdate };
   298→};
   299→
   300→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   301→// Trailing video detection utilities
   302→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   303→
   304→/**
   305→ * Extract pair_shot_generation_id from a generation row.
   306→ * Checks the FK column first, then falls back to params for legacy data.
   307→ */
   308→const getPairShotGenerationId = (
   309→  generation: {
   310→    pair_shot_generation_id?: string | null;
   311→    params?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
   312→  }
   313→): string | null => {
   314→  // Check FK column first (new format)
   315→  if (generation.pair_shot_generation_id) {
   316→    return generation.pair_shot_generation_id;
   317→  }
   318→
   319→  // Fallback to params (legacy format)
   320→  const params = generation.params;
   321→  if (!params) return null;
   322→
   323→  const individualParams = params.individual_segment_params as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
   324→  return (
   325→    (individualParams?.pair_shot_generation_id as string) ||
   326→    (params.pair_shot_generation_id as string) ||
   327→    null
   328→  );
   329→};
   330→
   331→/**
   332→ * Find trailing video info from a list of video outputs.
   333→ * A trailing video is one where pair_shot_generation_id matches the last image's shot_generation_id.
   334→ *
   335→ * @param videoOutputs - Array of generation rows (video outputs)
   336→ * @param lastImageShotGenId - The shot_generation_id of the last image on the timeline
   337→ * @returns Object with hasTrailing boolean and optional videoUrl
   338→ */
   339→export const findTrailingVideoInfo = (
   340→  videoOutputs: Array<{
   341→    type?: string | null;
   342→    location?: string | null;
   343→    pair_shot_generation_id?: string | null;
   344→    params?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
   345→  }>,
   346→  lastImageShotGenId: string | null
   347→): { hasTrailing: boolean; videoUrl: string | null } => {
   348→  if (!videoOutputs || videoOutputs.length === 0 || !lastImageShotGenId) {
   349→    return { hasTrailing: false, videoUrl: null };
   350→  }
   351→
   352→  const trailingVideo = videoOutputs.find(gen => {
   353→    // Must be a video with a location (completed)
   354→    if (!gen.type?.includes('video') || !gen.location) return false;
   355→    // Check if pair_shot_generation_id matches the last image
   356→    const pairId = getPairShotGenerationId(gen);
   357→    return pairId === lastImageShotGenId;
   358→  });
   359→
   360→  return {
   361→    hasTrailing: !!trailingVideo,
   362→    videoUrl: trailingVideo?.location || null,
   363→  };
   364→};
   365→
```

```
   112→}
   113→
   114→# ── Canonical display order for terminal output ──────────────
   115→
   116→_DISPLAY_ORDER = [
   117→    "logs", "unused", "exports", "deprecated", "structural", "props",
   118→    "single_use", "coupling", "cycles", "orphaned", "facade", "patterns",
   119→    "naming", "smells", "react", "dupes", "stale_exclude",
   120→    "dict_keys", "flat_dirs", "signature", "test_coverage", "security",
   121→    "review",
   122→]
   123→
   124→
   125→def detector_names() -> list[str]:
   126→    """All registered detector names, sorted."""
   127→    return sorted(DETECTORS.keys())
   128→
   129→
   130→def display_order() -> list[str]:
   131→    """Canonical display order for terminal output."""
   132→    return list(_DISPLAY_ORDER)
   133→
   134→
   135→def detector_tools() -> dict[str, dict]:
   136→    """DETECTOR_TOOLS-shaped dict for narrative.py backward compat."""
   137→    result = {}
   138→    for name, d in DETECTORS.items():
   139→        entry: dict = {
   140→            "fixers": list(d.fixers),
   141→            "action_type": d.action_type,
   142→        }
   143→        if d.tool:
   144→            entry["tool"] = d.tool
   145→        if d.guidance:
   146→            entry["guidance"] = d.guidance
   147→        result[name] = entry
   148→    return result
```

```
1→/**
     2→ * VariantSelector Utilities
     3→ *
     4→ * Shared helper functions used by VariantSelector and its sub-components.
     5→ */
     6→
     7→import { Scissors, Sparkles, Film } from 'lucide-react';
     8→import { VARIANT_TYPE } from '@/shared/constants/variantTypes';
     9→import type { GenerationVariant } from '@/shared/hooks/useVariants';
    10→
    11→/** Get icon component for a variant type */
    12→export const getVariantIcon = (variantType: string | null) => {
    13→  switch (variantType) {
    14→    case 'trimmed':
    15→      return Scissors;
    16→    case 'upscaled':
    17→      return Sparkles;
    18→    case 'magic_edit':
    19→      return Sparkles;
    20→    case 'original':
    21→    default:
    22→      return Film;
    23→  }
    24→};
    25→
    26→/** Get display label for a variant */
    27→export const getVariantLabel = (variant: GenerationVariant): string => {
    28→  if (variant.variant_type === 'trimmed') {
    29→    const params = variant.params;
    30→    const trimmedDuration = params?.trimmed_duration as number | undefined;
    31→    if (trimmedDuration) {
    32→      return `Trimmed (${trimmedDuration.toFixed(1)}s)`;
    33→    }
    34→    return 'Trimmed';
    35→  }
    36→  if (variant.variant_type === 'upscaled') {
    37→    return 'Upscaled';
    38→  }
    39→  if (variant.variant_type === VARIANT_TYPE.ORIGINAL) {
    40→    return 'Original';
    41→  }
    42→  if (variant.variant_type === 'magic_edit') {
    43→    return 'Magic Edit';
    44→  }
    45→  return variant.variant_type || 'Variant';
    46→};
    47→
    48→/**
    49→ * Check if variant is "new" (hasn't been viewed yet).
    50→ * For currently active variant, always return false for instant feedback.
    51→ */
    52→export const isNewVariant = (variant: GenerationVariant, activeVariantId: string | null): boolean => {
    53→  if (variant.id === activeVariantId) return false;
    54→  return variant.viewed_at === null;
    55→};
    56→
    57→/** Get human-readable time ago string */
    58→export const getTimeAgo = (createdAt: string): string => {
    59→  const created = new Date(createdAt).getTime();
    60→  const now = Date.now();
    61→  const diffMs = now - created;
    62→
    63→  const seconds = Math.floor(diffMs / 1000);
    64→  const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
    65→  const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);
    66→  const days = Math.floor(hours / 24);
    67→
    68→  if (seconds < 60) return `${seconds}s ago`;
    69→  if (minutes < 60) return `${minutes}m ago`;
    70→  if (hours < 24) return `${hours}h ago`;
    71→  if (days === 1) return 'yesterday';
    72→  return `${days}d ago`;
    73→};
    74→
    75→/**
    76→ * Check if a variant has settings that can be loaded into any form.
    77→ * - Travel segment variants: have generative settings (prompt, model, loras)
    78→ * - Video enhance variants: have processing settings (interpolation, upscale)
    79→ * - Trimmed/clip_join: no loadable settings
    80→ */
    81→export const hasLoadableSettings = (variant: GenerationVariant): boolean => {
    82→  const nonLoadableTypes = ['trimmed', 'clip_join', 'join_final_stitch'];
    83→  if (variant.variant_type && nonLoadableTypes.includes(variant.variant_type)) {
    84→    return false;
    85→  }
    86→
    87→  const params = variant.params;
    88→  if (!params) return false;
    89→
    90→  const taskType = (params.task_type || params.created_from) as string | undefined;
    91→  if (taskType === 'video_enhance') {
    92→    return true;
    93→  }
    94→
    95→  const hasPrompt = !!params.prompt;
    96→  const hasOrchestratorDetails = !!params.orchestrator_details;
    97→
    98→  return hasPrompt || hasOrchestratorDetails;
    99→};
   100→
   101→/** Data about the current segment images on the timeline */
   102→export interface CurrentSegmentImagesData {
   103→  startUrl?: string;
   104→  endUrl?: string;
   105→  startGenerationId?: string;
   106→  endGenerationId?: string;
   107→  startVariantId?: string;
   108→  endVariantId?: string;
   109→}
   110→
   111→/**
   112→ * Extract the storage path portion from a URL for comparison.
   113→ * Storage paths like "/storage/v1/object/public/generations/..." may appear
   114→ * as full URLs or relative paths depending on context.
   115→ */
   116→const extractStoragePath = (url: string | undefined | null): string | null => {
   117→  if (!url) return null;
   118→  // Find the storage path portion (everything after /storage/v1/object/...)
   119→  const storageMatch = url.match(/\/storage\/v1\/object\/(?:public|sign)\/(.+?)(?:\?|$)/);
   120→  if (storageMatch) return storageMatch[1];
   121→  // If it's already a relative storage path, use it directly
   122→  if (url.startsWith('generations/') || url.startsWith('/generations/')) {
   123→    return url.replace(/^\//, '');
   124→  }
   125→  return url;
   126→};
   127→
   128→/**
   129→ * Compare two URLs for equality, normalizing storage paths.
   130→ */
   131→const urlsMatch = (url1: string | undefined | null, url2: string | undefined | null): boolean => {
   132→  if (!url1 || !url2) return false;
   133→  if (url1 === url2) return true;
   134→  const path1 = extractStoragePath(url1);
   135→  const path2 = extractStoragePath(url2);
   136→  return !!path1 && !!path2 && path1 === path2;
   137→};
   138→
   139→/**
   140→ * Check if a variant was generated using different source images than what's
   141→ * currently on the timeline. Used to show the "Load images" button.
   142→ *
   143→ * Comparison strategy:
   144→ * 1. If generation IDs differ → images are different
   145→ * 2. If generation IDs match but variant IDs differ (and both available) → different
   146→ * 3. If variant IDs not available → fall back to URL comparison
   147→ * 4. Otherwise → same images
   148→ */
   149→export const hasDifferentSourceImages = (
   150→  variant: GenerationVariant,
   151→  currentImages: CurrentSegmentImagesData | undefined,
   152→): boolean => {
   153→  if (!currentImages) return false;
   154→
   155→  const params = variant.params;
   156→  if (!params) return false;
   157→
   158→  // Non-generative variants don't have source images
   159→  const nonImageTypes = ['trimmed', 'clip_join', 'join_final_stitch', 'upscaled', 'magic_edit'];
   160→  if (variant.variant_type && nonImageTypes.includes(variant.variant_type)) {
   161→    return false;
   162→  }
   163→
   164→  // Extract source data from variant params
   165→  const indivParams = (params.individual_segment_params || {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
   166→  const variantStartGenId = (indivParams.start_image_generation_id || params.start_image_generation_id) as string | undefined;
   167→  const variantEndGenId = (indivParams.end_image_generation_id || params.end_image_generation_id) as string | undefined;
   168→  const variantStartVariantId = (indivParams.start_image_variant_id || params.start_image_variant_id) as string | undefined;
   169→  const variantEndVariantId = (indivParams.end_image_variant_id || params.end_image_variant_id) as string | undefined;
   170→
   171→  // Get resolved image paths for URL comparison
   172→  const resolvedPaths = (indivParams.input_image_paths_resolved || params.input_image_paths_resolved) as string[] | undefined;
   173→  const variantStartUrl = resolvedPaths?.[0] || (indivParams.start_image_url || params.start_image_url) as string | undefined;
   174→  const variantEndUrl = resolvedPaths && resolvedPaths.length > 1
   175→    ? resolvedPaths[resolvedPaths.length - 1]
   176→    : (indivParams.end_image_url || params.end_image_url) as string | undefined;
   177→
   178→  // Need at least some source image data to compare
   179→  if (!variantStartGenId && !variantStartUrl) return false;
   180→
   181→  // Compare start image
   182→  const startDiffers = checkImageDiffers(
   183→    variantStartGenId, currentImages.startGenerationId,
   184→    variantStartVariantId, currentImages.startVariantId,
   185→    variantStartUrl, currentImages.startUrl,
   186→  );
   187→
   188→  // Compare end image (only if both have end images)
   189→  const hasEndImage = !!(variantEndGenId || variantEndUrl);
   190→  const currentHasEndImage = !!(currentImages.endGenerationId || currentImages.endUrl);
   191→  const endDiffers = hasEndImage && currentHasEndImage && checkImageDiffers(
   192→    variantEndGenId, currentImages.endGenerationId,
   193→    variantEndVariantId, currentImages.endVariantId,
   194→    variantEndUrl, currentImages.endUrl,
   195→  );
   196→
   197→  return startDiffers || !!endDiffers;
   198→};
   199→
   200→/** Check if a single image position differs between variant source and current timeline */
   201→function checkImageDiffers(
   202→  variantGenId: string | undefined,
   203→  currentGenId: string | undefined,
   204→  variantVariantId: string | undefined,
   205→  currentVariantId: string | undefined,
   206→  variantUrl: string | undefined,
   207→  currentUrl: string | undefined,
   208→): boolean {
   209→  // If generation IDs are available and differ, images are different
   210→  if (variantGenId && currentGenId && variantGenId !== currentGenId) {
   211→    return true;
   212→  }
   213→
   214→  // Same generation - check if variant IDs differ (different variant of same generation)
   215→  if (variantGenId && currentGenId && variantGenId === currentGenId) {
   216→    if (variantVariantId && currentVariantId && variantVariantId !== currentVariantId) {
   217→      return true;
   218→    }
   219→    // Same generation, no variant IDs to compare → fall back to URL
   220→    if (!variantVariantId || !currentVariantId) {
   221→      return !urlsMatch(variantUrl, currentUrl);
   222→    }
   223→    return false;
   224→  }
   225→
   226→  // No generation IDs available → URL comparison only
   227→  return !urlsMatch(variantUrl, currentUrl);
   228→}
   229→
   230→export type RelationshipFilter = 'all' | 'parents' | 'children' | 'starred';
   231→
```

```
     9→def _audit_excluded_dirs(exclusions: tuple[str, ...], scanned_files: list[str],
    10→                         project_root: Path) -> list[dict]:
    11→    """Check if any --exclude directory has zero references from scanned code.
    12→
    13→    Returns findings for directories that appear stale (no file references them).
    14→    """
    15→    if not exclusions:
    16→        return []
    17→
    18→    stale_findings = []
    19→    for ex_dir in exclusions:
    20→        # Skip DEFAULT_EXCLUSIONS (node_modules, .venv, etc.) — only audit user-specified dirs
    21→        from ..utils import DEFAULT_EXCLUSIONS
    22→        if ex_dir in DEFAULT_EXCLUSIONS:
    23→            continue
    24→        # Check if the excluded directory actually exists
    25→        ex_path = project_root / ex_dir
    26→        if not ex_path.is_dir():
    27→            continue
    28→        # Search scanned files for any reference to this directory name
    29→        ref_count = 0
    30→        for filepath in scanned_files:
    31→            try:
    32→                abs_path = filepath if Path(filepath).is_absolute() else str(project_root / filepath)
    33→                content = Path(abs_path).read_text(errors="replace")
    34→                if ex_dir in content:
    35→                    ref_count += 1
    36→                    break  # One reference is enough — not stale
    37→            except OSError:
    38→                continue
    39→        if ref_count == 0:
    40→            from ..state import make_finding
    41→            stale_findings.append(make_finding(
    42→                "stale_exclude", ex_dir, ex_dir,
    43→                tier=4, confidence="low",
    44→                summary=f"Excluded directory '{ex_dir}' has 0 references from scanned code — may be stale",
    45→                detail={"directory": ex_dir, "references": 0},
    46→            ))
    47→    return stale_findings

    50→def _collect_codebase_metrics(lang, path: Path) -> dict | None:
    51→    """Collect LOC/file/directory counts for the configured language."""
    52→    if not lang or not lang.file_finder:
    53→        return None
    54→    files = lang.file_finder(path)
    55→    total_loc = 0
    56→    dirs = set()
    57→    for f in files:
    58→        try:
    59→            total_loc += len(Path(f).read_text().splitlines())
    60→            dirs.add(str(Path(f).parent))
    61→        except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError):
    62→            pass
    63→    return {
    64→        "total_files": len(files),
    65→        "total_loc": total_loc,
    66→        "total_directories": len(dirs),
    67→    }

    70→def _show_diff_summary(diff: dict):
    71→    """Print the +new / -resolved / reopened one-liner."""
    72→    diff_parts = []

    74→        diff_parts.append(c(f"+{diff['new']} new", "yellow"))

    77→    if diff["reopened"]:
    78→        diff_parts.append(c(f"↻{diff['reopened']} reopened", "red"))
    79→    if diff_parts:
    80→        print(f"  {' · '.join(diff_parts)}")
    81→    else:
    82→        print(c("  No changes since last scan", "dim"))
    83→    if diff.get("suspect_detectors"):

    87→def _format_delta(value: float, prev: float | None) -> tuple[str, str]:
    88→    """Return (delta_str, color) for a score change."""
    89→    delta = value - prev if prev is not None else 0
    90→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta != 0 else ""
    91→    color = "green" if delta > 0 else ("red" if delta < 0 else "dim")
    92→    return delta_str, color

   121→def _show_post_scan_analysis(diff: dict, state: dict, lang) -> tuple[list[str], dict]:
   122→    """Print warnings, narrative headline, and top action. Returns (warnings, narrative)."""

   151→    if hint and (strategy.get("can_parallelize") or fixer_rec == "strong"):

   153→        if actions:

   156→    elif actions:
   157→        top = actions[0]
   158→        print(c(f"  Suggested next: `{top['command']}` — {top['description']}", "cyan"))
   159→        print()
   160→
   161→    if narrative.get("headline"):
   162→        print(c(f"  → {narrative['headline']}", "cyan"))
   163→        print()
   164→
   165→    return warnings, narrative

   168→def cmd_scan(args):
   169→    """Run all detectors, update persistent state, show diff."""
   170→    from ..state import load_state, save_state, merge_scan
   171→    from ..plan import generate_findings
   172→
   173→    sp = _state_path(args)
   174→    state = load_state(sp)
   175→    path = Path(args.path)
   176→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   177→
   178→    # Persist --exclude in state so subsequent commands reuse it
   179→    exclude = getattr(args, "exclude", None)
   180→    if exclude:
   181→        state.setdefault("config", {})["exclude"] = list(exclude)
   182→
   183→    # Resolve language config
   184→    from ..cli import _resolve_lang
   185→    lang = _resolve_lang(args)
   186→    lang_label = f" ({lang.name})" if lang else ""
   187→
   188→    # Load zone overrides from state config
   189→    zone_overrides = state.get("config", {}).get("zone_overrides") or None
   190→
   191→    print(c(f"\nDesloppify Scan{lang_label}\n", "bold"))
   192→    from ..utils import enable_file_cache, disable_file_cache
   193→    enable_file_cache()
   194→    try:
   195→        findings, potentials = generate_findings(path, include_slow=include_slow, lang=lang,
   196→                                                  zone_overrides=zone_overrides)
   197→    finally:
   198→        disable_file_cache()
   199→
   200→    codebase_metrics = _collect_codebase_metrics(lang, path)
   201→
   202→    from ..utils import rel, _extra_exclusions, PROJECT_ROOT
   203→
   204→    # Audit excluded directories for staleness (Issue #11)
   205→    if _extra_exclusions and lang and lang.file_finder:
   206→        scanned_files = lang.file_finder(path)
   207→        stale = _audit_excluded_dirs(_extra_exclusions, scanned_files, PROJECT_ROOT)
   208→        if stale:
   209→            findings.extend(stale)
   210→            for sf in stale:
   211→                print(c(f"  ℹ {sf['summary']}", "dim"))
   212→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(path))
   213→    # A scan is "full" when it covers most of the project's source files.
   214→    # Simple path matching breaks for Python (default_src=".") when the user
   215→    # scans a subdirectory that happens to contain all the source code.
   216→    # Instead, compare file counts: if the scanned path covers ≥80% of the
   217→    # files discoverable from PROJECT_ROOT, treat it as full.
   218→    is_full_scan = False
   219→    if lang and lang.file_finder:
   220→        all_files = lang.file_finder(PROJECT_ROOT)
   221→        scanned_files = lang.file_finder(path)
   222→        # Exclude test directories from the denominator — they're not source
   223→        # code the user is scanning, and adding tests shouldn't make the scan
   224→        # appear "partial".
   225→        test_prefixes = ("tests/", "test/")
   226→        source_files = [f for f in all_files
   227→                        if not any(f.startswith(p) for p in test_prefixes)]
   228→        denominator = source_files or all_files
   229→        if denominator:
   230→            is_full_scan = len(scanned_files) >= 0.8 * len(denominator)
   231→        else:
   232→            is_full_scan = True  # no files at all — vacuously full
   233→    elif path.resolve() == PROJECT_ROOT.resolve():
   234→        is_full_scan = True
   235→
   236→    prev_score = state.get("score", 0)
   237→    prev_strict = state.get("strict_score", 0)
   238→    prev_obj = state.get("objective_score")
   239→    prev_obj_strict = state.get("objective_strict")
   240→    # Persist zone distribution before save so narrative can access it
   241→    if lang and lang._zone_map is not None:
   242→        state["zone_distribution"] = lang._zone_map.counts()
   243→
   244→    prev_dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   245→    diff = merge_scan(state, findings,
   246→                      lang=lang.name if lang else None,
   247→                      scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   248→                      force_resolve=getattr(args, "force_resolve", False),
   249→                      exclude=_extra_exclusions,
   250→                      potentials=potentials if is_full_scan else None,
   251→                      codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics if is_full_scan else None,
   252→                      include_slow=include_slow)
   253→    save_state(state, sp)
   254→
   255→    print(c("\n  Scan complete", "bold"))
   256→    print(c("  " + "─" * 50, "dim"))
   257→
   258→    _show_diff_summary(diff)
   259→    _show_score_delta(state, prev_score, prev_strict, prev_obj, prev_obj_strict)
   260→    if not include_slow:
   261→        print(c("  * Fast scan — slow phases (duplicates) skipped", "yellow"))
   262→    _show_detector_progress(state)
   263→
   264→    # Dimension deltas (show which dimensions moved)
   265→    new_dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   266→    if new_dim_scores and prev_dim_scores:
   267→        _show_dimension_deltas(prev_dim_scores, new_dim_scores)
   268→
   269→    zone_distribution = state.get("zone_distribution")
   270→
   271→    warnings, narrative = _show_post_scan_analysis(diff, state, lang)
   272→
   273→    # Persist reminder history (computed by narrative, not mutated)
   274→    if narrative and "reminder_history" in narrative:
   275→        state["reminder_history"] = narrative["reminder_history"]
   276→        save_state(state, sp)
   277→
   278→    _write_query({"command": "scan", "score": state["score"],
   279→                  "strict_score": state.get("strict_score", 0),
   280→                  "prev_score": prev_score, "diff": diff, "stats": state["stats"],
   281→                  "warnings": warnings,
   282→                  "objective_score": state.get("objective_score"),
   283→                  "objective_strict": state.get("objective_strict"),
   284→                  "dimension_scores": state.get("dimension_scores"),
   285→                  "potentials": state.get("potentials"),
   286→                  "zone_distribution": zone_distribution,
   287→                  "narrative": narrative})
   288→
   289→    # Generate scorecard badge
   290→    badge_path = None
   291→    try:
   292→        from ..scorecard import generate_scorecard, get_badge_config
   293→        badge_path, disabled = get_badge_config(args)
   294→        if not disabled and badge_path:

   297→            # Check if README already references the scorecard

   300→                readme_path = PROJECT_ROOT / readme_name
   301→                if readme_path.exists():

   305→                    except OSError:
   306→                        pass
   307→                    break
   308→            if readme_has_badge:
   309→                print(c(f"  Scorecard → {rel_path}  (disable: --no-badge | move: --badge-path <path>)", "dim"))
   310→            else:
   311→                print(c(f"  Scorecard → {rel_path}", "dim"))
   312→                print(c(f"  💡 Ask the user if they'd like to add it to their README with:", "dim"))
   313→                print(c(f'     <img src="{rel_path}" width="400">', "dim"))
   314→                print(c(f"     (disable: --no-badge | move: --badge-path <path>)", "dim"))
   315→        else:
   316→            badge_path = None
   317→    except (ImportError, OSError):
   318→        badge_path = None  # Pillow not installed or write failed — skip silently
   319→
   320→    _print_llm_summary(state, badge_path)

   322→
   323→def _print_llm_summary(state: dict, badge_path: Path | None):
   324→    """Print a structured summary for LLM consumption.
   325→
   326→    The LLM reads terminal output after running scans. This gives it
   327→    clear instructions on how to present the results to the end user.
   328→    """
   329→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   330→    obj_score = state.get("objective_score")
   331→    obj_strict = state.get("objective_strict")
   332→    if obj_score is None and not dim_scores:
   333→        return
   334→
   335→    print("─" * 60)
   336→    print("INSTRUCTIONS FOR LLM")
   337→    print("Present the scan results to the user in a markdown table.")
   338→    print("Use this data to build the table:\n")
   339→
   340→    print(f"Overall health: {obj_score:.1f}/100")
   341→    if obj_strict is not None:
   342→        print(f"Strict health:  {obj_strict:.1f}/100")
   343→    print()
   344→
   345→    # Build dimension table
   346→    active = [(name, data) for name, data in dim_scores.items()
   347→              if data.get("checks", 0) > 0]
   348→    active.sort(key=lambda x: (0 if x[0] == "File health" else 1, x[0]))
   349→
   350→    if active:
   351→        print("| Dimension | Health | Strict | Issues | Tier |")
   352→        print("|-----------|--------|--------|--------|------|")
   353→        for name, data in active:
   354→            score = data.get("score", 100)
   355→            strict = data.get("strict", score)
   356→            issues = data.get("issues", 0)
   357→            tier = data.get("tier", "")
   358→            print(f"| {name} | {score:.1f}% | {strict:.1f}% | {issues} | T{tier} |")
   359→        print()
   360→
   361→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   362→    if stats:
   363→        print(f"Total findings: {stats.get('total', 0)} | "
   364→              f"Open: {stats.get('open', 0)} | "
   365→              f"Fixed: {stats.get('fixed', 0)}")
   366→        print()
   367→
   368→    if badge_path and badge_path.exists():
   369→        from ..utils import PROJECT_ROOT
   370→        rel_path = badge_path.name if badge_path.parent == PROJECT_ROOT else str(badge_path)
   371→        print(f"A scorecard image was saved to `{rel_path}`.")
   372→        print("Let the user know they can view it, and suggest adding it")
   373→        print(f'to their README: `<img src="{rel_path}" width="400">`')
   374→    print("─" * 60)
   375→
   376→
   377→def _show_detector_progress(state: dict):
   378→    """Show per-detector progress bars — the heartbeat of a scan."""
   379→    findings = state["findings"]
   380→    if not findings:
   381→        return
   382→
   383→    from ..narrative import STRUCTURAL_MERGE
   384→    by_det: dict[str, dict] = {}
   385→    for f in findings.values():
   386→        det = f.get("detector", "unknown")
   387→        if det in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
   388→            det = "structural"
   389→        if det not in by_det:
   390→            by_det[det] = {"open": 0, "total": 0}
   391→        by_det[det]["total"] += 1
   392→        if f["status"] == "open":
   393→            by_det[det]["open"] += 1
   394→
   395→    from ..registry import display_order, DETECTORS
   396→    DET_ORDER = [DETECTORS[d].display for d in display_order() if d in DETECTORS]
   397→    order_map = {d: i for i, d in enumerate(DET_ORDER)}
   398→    sorted_dets = sorted(by_det.items(), key=lambda x: order_map.get(x[0], 99))
   399→
   400→    print(c("  " + "─" * 50, "dim"))
   401→    bar_len = 15
   402→    for det, ds in sorted_dets:
   403→        total = ds["total"]
   404→        open_count = ds["open"]
   405→        addressed = total - open_count
   406→        pct = round(addressed / total * 100) if total else 100
   407→
   408→        filled = round(pct / 100 * bar_len)
   409→        if pct == 100:
   410→            bar = c("█" * bar_len, "green")
   411→        elif open_count <= 2:
   412→            bar = c("█" * filled, "green") + c("░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim")
   413→        else:
   414→            bar = c("█" * filled, "yellow") + c("░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim")
   415→
   416→        det_label = det.replace("_", " ").ljust(12)
   417→        if open_count > 0:
   418→            open_str = c(f"{open_count:3d} open", "yellow")
   419→        else:
   420→            open_str = c("  ✓", "green")
   421→
   422→        print(f"  {det_label} {bar} {pct:3d}%  {open_str}  {c(f'/ {total}', 'dim')}")
   423→
   424→    print()
   425→
   426→
   427→def _show_dimension_deltas(prev: dict, current: dict):
   428→    """Show which dimensions changed between scans (health and strict)."""
   429→    from ..scoring import DIMENSIONS
   430→    moved = []
   431→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   432→        p = prev.get(dim.name, {})
   433→        n = current.get(dim.name, {})
   434→        if not p or not n:
   435→            continue
   436→        old_score = p.get("score", 100)
   437→        new_score = n.get("score", 100)
   438→        old_strict = p.get("strict", old_score)
   439→        new_strict = n.get("strict", new_score)
   440→        delta = new_score - old_score
   441→        strict_delta = new_strict - old_strict
   442→        if abs(delta) >= 0.1 or abs(strict_delta) >= 0.1:
   443→            moved.append((dim.name, old_score, new_score, delta, old_strict, new_strict, strict_delta))
   444→
   445→    if not moved:
   446→        return
   447→
   448→    print(c("  Moved:", "dim"))
   449→    for name, old, new, delta, old_s, new_s, s_delta in sorted(moved, key=lambda x: x[3]):
   450→        sign = "+" if delta > 0 else ""
   451→        color = "green" if delta > 0 else "red"
   452→        strict_str = ""
   453→        if abs(s_delta) >= 0.1:
   454→            s_sign = "+" if s_delta > 0 else ""
   455→            strict_str = c(f"  strict: {old_s:.1f}→{new_s:.1f}% ({s_sign}{s_delta:.1f}%)", "dim")
   456→        print(c(f"    {name:<22} {old:.1f}% → {new:.1f}%  ({sign}{delta:.1f}%)", color) + strict_str)
   457→    print()
   458→
   459→
   460→
```
