   122→def _detect_async_no_await(filepath: str, content: str, lines: list[str],
   123→                           smell_counts: dict[str, list[dict]]):
   124→    """Find async functions that don't use await.
   125→
   126→    Algorithm: for each async declaration, track brace depth to find the function
   127→    body extent (up to 200 lines). Scan each line for 'await' within those braces.
   128→    If the opening brace closes (depth returns to 0) without seeing await, flag it.
   129→    """
   130→    async_re = re.compile(r"(?:async\s+function\s+(\w+)|(\w+)\s*=\s*async)")
   131→    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
   132→        m = async_re.search(line)
   133→        if not m:
   134→            continue
   135→        name = m.group(1) or m.group(2)
   136→        brace_depth = 0
   137→        found_open = False
   138→        has_await = False
   139→        for j in range(i, min(i + 200, len(lines))):
   140→            body_line = lines[j]
   141→            prev_code_ch = ""
   142→            for _, ch, in_s in _scan_code(body_line):
   143→                if in_s:
   144→                    continue
   145→                if ch == '/' and prev_code_ch == '/':
   146→                    break  # Rest of line is comment
   147→                elif ch == '{':
   148→                    brace_depth += 1
   149→                    found_open = True
   150→                elif ch == '}':
   151→                    brace_depth -= 1
   152→                prev_code_ch = ch
   153→            if "await " in body_line or "await\n" in body_line:
   154→                has_await = True
   155→            if found_open and brace_depth <= 0:
   156→                break
   157→
   158→        if found_open and not has_await:
   159→            smell_counts["async_no_await"].append({
   160→                "file": filepath,
   161→                "line": i + 1,
   162→                "content": f"async {name or '(anonymous)'} has no await",
   163→            })
   164→
   165→
   166→def _detect_error_no_throw(filepath: str, lines: list[str],
   167→                           smell_counts: dict[str, list[dict]]):
   168→    """Find console.error calls not followed by throw or return."""
   169→    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
   170→        if "console.error" in line:
   171→            following = "\n".join(lines[i+1:i+4])
   172→            if not re.search(r"\b(?:throw|return)\b", following):
   173→                smell_counts["console_error_no_throw"].append({
   174→                    "file": filepath,
   175→                    "line": i + 1,
   176→                    "content": line.strip()[:100],
   177→                })
   178→
   179→
   180→def _detect_empty_if_chains(filepath: str, lines: list[str],
   181→                            smell_counts: dict[str, list[dict]]):
   182→    """Find if/else chains where all branches are empty."""
   183→    i = 0
   184→    while i < len(lines):
   185→        stripped = lines[i].strip()
   186→        if not re.match(r"(?:else\s+)?if\s*\(", stripped):
   187→            i += 1
   188→            continue
   189→
   190→        # Single-line: if (...) { }
   191→        if re.match(r"(?:else\s+)?if\s*\([^)]*\)\s*\{\s*\}\s*$", stripped):
   192→            chain_start = i
   193→            j = i + 1
   194→            while j < len(lines):
   195→                next_stripped = lines[j].strip()
   196→                if re.match(r"else\s+if\s*\([^)]*\)\s*\{\s*\}\s*$", next_stripped):
   197→                    j += 1
   198→                    continue
   199→                if re.match(r"(?:\}\s*)?else\s*\{\s*\}\s*$", next_stripped):
   200→                    j += 1
   201→                    continue
   202→                break
   203→            smell_counts["empty_if_chain"].append({
   204→                "file": filepath,
   205→                "line": chain_start + 1,
   206→                "content": stripped[:100],
   207→            })
   208→            i = j
   209→            continue
   210→
   211→        # Multi-line: if (...) { followed by } on next non-blank line
   212→        if re.match(r"(?:else\s+)?if\s*\([^)]*\)\s*\{\s*$", stripped):
   213→            chain_start = i
   214→            chain_all_empty = True
   215→            j = i
   216→            while j < len(lines):
   217→                cur = lines[j].strip()
   218→                if j == chain_start:
   219→                    if not re.match(r"(?:else\s+)?if\s*\([^)]*\)\s*\{\s*$", cur):
   220→                        chain_all_empty = False
   221→                        break
   222→                elif re.match(r"\}\s*else\s+if\s*\([^)]*\)\s*\{\s*$", cur):
   223→                    pass
   224→                elif re.match(r"\}\s*else\s*\{\s*$", cur):
   225→                    pass
   226→                elif cur == "}":
   227→                    k = j + 1
   228→                    while k < len(lines) and lines[k].strip() == "":
   229→                        k += 1
   230→                    if k < len(lines) and re.match(r"else\s", lines[k].strip()):
   231→                        j = k
   232→                        continue
   233→                    j += 1
   234→                    break
   235→                elif cur == "":
   236→                    j += 1
   237→                    continue
   238→                else:
   239→                    chain_all_empty = False
   240→                    break
   241→                j += 1
   242→
   243→            if chain_all_empty and j > chain_start + 1:
   244→                smell_counts["empty_if_chain"].append({
   245→                    "file": filepath,
   246→                    "line": chain_start + 1,
   247→                    "content": lines[chain_start].strip()[:100],
   248→                })
   249→            i = max(i + 1, j)
   250→            continue
   251→
   252→        i += 1
   253→
   254→
   255→def _detect_dead_useeffects(filepath: str, lines: list[str],
   256→                            smell_counts: dict[str, list[dict]]):
   257→    """Find useEffect calls with empty or whitespace/comment-only bodies.
   258→
   259→    Algorithm: two-pass brace/paren tracking with string-escape awareness.
   260→    Pass 1: track paren depth to find the full useEffect(...) extent.
   261→    Pass 2: within that extent, find the arrow body ({...} after =>) using
   262→    brace depth, skipping characters inside string literals (', ", `).
   263→    Then strip comments from the body and check if anything remains.
   264→    """
   265→    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
   266→        stripped = line.strip()
   267→        if not re.match(r"(?:React\.)?useEffect\s*\(\s*\(\s*\)\s*=>\s*\{", stripped):
   268→            continue
   269→
   270→        paren_depth = 0
   271→        brace_depth = 0
   272→        end = None
   273→        for j in range(i, min(i + 30, len(lines))):
   274→            for _, ch, in_s in _scan_code(lines[j]):
   275→                if in_s:
   276→                    continue
   277→                if ch == "(":
   278→                    paren_depth += 1
   279→                elif ch == ")":
   280→                    paren_depth -= 1
   281→                    if paren_depth <= 0:
   282→                        end = j
   283→                        break
   284→                elif ch == "{":
   285→                    brace_depth += 1
   286→                elif ch == "}":
   287→                    brace_depth -= 1
   288→            if end is not None:
   289→                break
   290→
   291→        if end is None:
   292→            continue
   293→
   294→        text = "\n".join(lines[i:end + 1])
   295→        arrow_pos = text.find("=>")
   296→        if arrow_pos == -1:
   297→            continue
   298→        brace_pos = text.find("{", arrow_pos)
   299→        if brace_pos == -1:
   300→            continue
   301→
   302→        depth = 0
   303→        body_end = None
   304→        for ci, ch, in_s in _scan_code(text, brace_pos):
   305→            if in_s:
   306→                continue
   307→            if ch == "{":
   308→                depth += 1
   309→            elif ch == "}":
   310→                depth -= 1
   311→                if depth == 0:
   312→                    body_end = ci
   313→                    break
   314→
   315→        if body_end is None:
   316→            continue
   317→
   318→        body = text[brace_pos + 1:body_end]
   319→        body_stripped = _strip_ts_comments(body)
   320→        if body_stripped.strip() == "":
   321→            smell_counts["dead_useeffect"].append({
   322→                "file": filepath,
   323→                "line": i + 1,
   324→                "content": stripped[:100],
   325→            })
   326→
   327→
   328→def _detect_swallowed_errors(filepath: str, content: str, lines: list[str],
   329→                              smell_counts: dict[str, list[dict]]):
   330→    """Find catch blocks whose only content is console.error/warn/log (swallowed errors).
   331→
   332→    Algorithm: regex-find each `catch(...) {`, then track brace depth with
   333→    string-escape awareness to extract the catch body (up to 500 chars).
   334→    Strip comments, split into statements, and check if every statement
   335→    is a console.error/warn/log call.
   336→    """
   337→    catch_re = re.compile(r"catch\s*\([^)]*\)\s*\{")
   338→    for m in catch_re.finditer(content):
   339→        brace_start = m.end() - 1
   340→        depth = 0
   341→        body_end = None
   342→        for ci, ch, in_s in _scan_code(content, brace_start, min(brace_start + 500, len(content))):
   343→            if in_s:
   344→                continue
   345→            if ch == "{":
   346→                depth += 1
   347→            elif ch == "}":
   348→                depth -= 1
   349→                if depth == 0:
   350→                    body_end = ci
   351→                    break
   352→
   353→        if body_end is None:
   354→            continue
   355→
   356→        body = content[brace_start + 1:body_end]
   357→        body_clean = _strip_ts_comments(body).strip()
   358→
   359→        if not body_clean:
   360→            continue  # empty catch — caught by empty_catch detector
   361→
   362→        statements = [s.strip().rstrip(";") for s in re.split(r"[;\n]", body_clean) if s.strip()]
   363→        if not statements:
   364→            continue
   365→
   366→        all_console = all(
   367→            re.match(r"console\.(error|warn|log)\s*\(", stmt)
   368→            for stmt in statements
   369→        )
   370→        if all_console:
   371→            line_no = content[:m.start()].count("\n") + 1
   372→            smell_counts["swallowed_error"].append({
   373→                "file": filepath,
   374→                "line": line_no,
   375→                "content": lines[line_no - 1].strip()[:100] if line_no <= len(lines) else "",
   376→            })
   377→
   378→
   379→def _track_brace_body(lines: list[str], start_line: int, *, max_scan: int = 2000) -> int | None:
   380→    """Find the closing brace that matches the first opening brace from start_line.
   381→
   382→    Tracks brace depth with string-literal awareness (', ", `).
   383→    Returns the line index of the closing brace, or None if not found.
   384→    """
   385→    depth = 0
   386→    found_open = False
   387→    for j in range(start_line, min(start_line + max_scan, len(lines))):
   388→        for _, ch, in_s in _scan_code(lines[j]):
   389→            if in_s:
   390→                continue
   391→            if ch == "{":
   392→                depth += 1
   393→                found_open = True
   394→            elif ch == "}":
   395→                depth -= 1
   396→                if found_open and depth == 0:
   397→                    return j
   398→    return None
   399→
   400→
   401→def _find_function_start(line: str, next_lines: list[str]) -> str | None:
   402→    """Return the function name if this line starts a named function, else None.
   403→
   404→    Matches:
   405→    - function foo(...)
   406→    - export function foo(...)
   407→    - export default function foo(...)
   408→    - const foo = (...) => {
   409→    - const foo = async (...) => {
   410→    - const foo = function(...)
   411→
   412→    Skips interfaces, types, enums, classes.
   413→    """
   414→    stripped = line.strip()
   415→    if re.match(r"(?:export\s+)?(?:interface|type|enum|class)\s+", stripped):
   416→        return None
   417→
   418→    # function declaration
   419→    m = re.match(
   420→        r"(?:export\s+)?(?:default\s+)?(?:async\s+)?function\s+(\w+)\s*\(",
   421→        stripped,
   422→    )
   423→    if m:
   424→        return m.group(1)
   425→
   426→    # const/let/var assignment — need to verify it's actually a function
   427→    m = re.match(r"(?:export\s+)?(?:const|let|var)\s+(\w+)", stripped)
   428→    if not m:
   429→        return None
   430→    name = m.group(1)
   431→
   432→    # Look at what follows the = sign
   433→    combined = "\n".join([stripped] + [l.strip() for l in next_lines[:2]])
   434→    eq_pos = combined.find("=", m.end())
   435→    if eq_pos == -1:
   436→        return None
   437→    after_eq = combined[eq_pos + 1:].lstrip()
   438→
   439→    # Direct function: must start with (, async, or function keyword.
   440→    # Skips function calls like useMemo(...), useRef(...), React.memo(...) that
   441→    # may contain => in their arguments or type annotations.
   442→    if re.match(r"(?:async|function)\b", after_eq):
   443→        return name
   444→    if after_eq.startswith("("):
   445→        # Check that this ( ... ) is followed by => (arrow function params)
   446→        # not just a function call like someFunction(...)
   447→        brace_pos = combined.find("{", eq_pos)
   448→        segment = combined[eq_pos:brace_pos] if brace_pos != -1 else combined[eq_pos:]
   449→        if "=>" in segment:
   450→            return name
   451→    return None
   452→
   453→
   454→def _detect_monster_functions(filepath: str, lines: list[str],
   455→                              smell_counts: dict[str, list[dict]]):
   456→    """Find functions/components exceeding 150 LOC via brace-tracking.
   457→
   458→    Matches: function declarations, named arrow functions, and React components.
   459→    Skips: interfaces, types, enums, and objects/arrays.
   460→    """
   461→    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
   462→        name = _find_function_start(line, lines[i + 1:i + 3])
   463→        if not name:
   464→            continue
   465→
   466→        # Find opening brace on this or next few lines
   467→        brace_line = None
   468→        for k in range(i, min(i + 5, len(lines))):
   469→            if "{" in lines[k]:
   470→                brace_line = k
   471→                break
   472→        if brace_line is None:
   473→            continue
   474→
   475→        end_line = _track_brace_body(lines, brace_line, max_scan=2000)
   476→        if end_line is not None:
   477→            loc = end_line - i + 1
   478→            if loc > 150:
   479→                smell_counts["monster_function"].append({
   480→                    "file": filepath,
   481→                    "line": i + 1,
   482→                    "content": f"{name}() — {loc} LOC",
   483→                })
   484→
   485→
   486→def _detect_dead_functions(filepath: str, lines: list[str],
   487→                           smell_counts: dict[str, list[dict]]):
   488→    """Find functions with empty body or only return/return null.
   489→
   490→    Matches function declarations and named arrow functions.
   491→    Skips decorated functions (TS decorators on line above).
   492→    """
   493→    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
   494→        # Skip decorated functions
   495→        if i > 0 and lines[i - 1].strip().startswith("@"):
   496→            continue
   497→
   498→        name = _find_function_start(line, lines[i + 1:i + 3])
   499→        if not name:
   500→            continue
   501→
   502→        # Find opening brace
   503→        brace_line = None
   504→        for k in range(i, min(i + 5, len(lines))):
   505→            if "{" in lines[k]:
   506→                brace_line = k
   507→                break
   508→        if brace_line is None:
   509→            continue
   510→
   511→        end_line = _track_brace_body(lines, brace_line, max_scan=30)
   512→        if end_line is None:
   513→            continue
   514→
   515→        # Extract body between braces
   516→        body_text = "\n".join(lines[brace_line:end_line + 1])
   517→        first_brace = body_text.find("{")
   518→        last_brace = body_text.rfind("}")
   519→        if first_brace == -1 or last_brace == -1 or first_brace >= last_brace:
   520→            continue
   521→
   522→        body = body_text[first_brace + 1:last_brace]
   523→        body_clean = _strip_ts_comments(body).strip().rstrip(";")
   524→
   525→        if body_clean in ("", "return", "return null", "return undefined"):
   526→            label = body_clean or "empty"
   527→            smell_counts["dead_function"].append({
   528→                "file": filepath,
   529→                "line": i + 1,
   530→                "content": f"{name}() — body is {label}",
   531→            })
   532→
   533→
   534→def _detect_window_globals(filepath: str, lines: list[str],
   535→                           line_state: dict[int, str],
   536→                           smell_counts: dict[str, list[dict]]):
   537→    """Find window.__* assignments — global state escape hatches.
   538→
   539→    Matches:
   540→    - window.__foo = ...
   541→    - (window as any).__foo = ...
   542→    - window['__foo'] = ...
   543→    """
   544→    window_re = re.compile(
   545→        r"""(?:"""
   546→        r"""\(?\s*window\s+as\s+any\s*\)?\s*\.\s*(__\w+)"""   # (window as any).__name
   547→        r"""|window\s*\.\s*(__\w+)"""                           # window.__name
   548→        r"""|window\s*\[\s*['"](__\w+)['"]\s*\]"""              # window['__name']
   549→        r""")\s*=""",
   550→    )
   551→    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
   552→        if i in line_state:
   553→            continue
   554→        m = window_re.search(line)
   555→        if not m:
   556→            continue
   557→        if _ts_match_is_in_string(line, m.start()):
   558→            continue
   559→        smell_counts["window_global"].append({
   560→            "file": filepath,
   561→            "line": i + 1,
   562→            "content": line.strip()[:100],
   563→        })
   564→
   565→
   566→def _detect_catch_return_default(filepath: str, content: str,
   567→                                  smell_counts: dict[str, list[dict]]):
   568→    """Find catch blocks that return object literals with default/no-op values.
   569→
   570→    Catches the pattern:
   571→      catch (...) { ... return { key: false, key: null, key: () => {} }; }
   572→
   573→    This is a silent failure — the caller gets valid-looking data but the
   574→    operation actually failed.
   575→    """
   576→    catch_re = re.compile(r"catch\s*\([^)]*\)\s*\{")
   577→    for m in catch_re.finditer(content):
   578→        brace_start = m.end() - 1
   579→        depth = 0
   580→        body_end = None
   581→        for ci, ch, in_s in _scan_code(content, brace_start, min(brace_start + _MAX_CATCH_BODY, len(content))):
   582→            if in_s:
   583→                continue
   584→            if ch == "{":
   585→                depth += 1
   586→            elif ch == "}":
   587→                depth -= 1
   588→                if depth == 0:
   589→                    body_end = ci
   590→                    break
   591→
   592→        if body_end is None:
   593→            continue
   594→
   595→        body = content[brace_start + 1:body_end]
   596→        # Check if body contains "return {" — a return with object literal
   597→        return_obj = re.search(r"\breturn\s*\{", body)
   598→        if not return_obj:
   599→            continue
   600→
   601→        # Extract the returned object content
   602→        obj_start = body.find("{", return_obj.start())
   603→        obj_depth = 0
   604→        obj_end = None
   605→        for ci, ch, in_s in _scan_code(body, obj_start):
   606→            if in_s:
   607→                continue
   608→            if ch == "{":
   609→                obj_depth += 1
   610→            elif ch == "}":
   611→                obj_depth -= 1
   612→                if obj_depth == 0:
   613→                    obj_end = ci
   614→                    break
   615→
   616→        if obj_end is None:
   617→            continue
   618→
   619→        obj_content = body[obj_start + 1:obj_end]
   620→        # Count default/no-op fields
   621→        noop_count = len(re.findall(r"\(\)\s*=>\s*\{\s*\}", obj_content))  # () => {}
   622→        false_count = len(re.findall(r":\s*(?:false|null|undefined|0|''|\"\")\b", obj_content))
   623→        default_fields = noop_count + false_count
   624→
   625→        if default_fields >= 2:
   626→            line_no = content[:m.start()].count("\n") + 1
   627→            # Use content slice to get the line without re-splitting entire file
   628→            line_start = content.rfind("\n", 0, m.start()) + 1
   629→            line_end = content.find("\n", m.start())
   630→            if line_end == -1:
   631→                line_end = len(content)
   632→            smell_counts["catch_return_default"].append({
   633→                "file": filepath,
   634→                "line": line_no,
   635→                "content": content[line_start:line_end].strip()[:100],
   636→            })
   637→
   638→
   639→def _detect_switch_no_default(filepath: str, content: str,
   640→                               smell_counts: dict[str, list[dict]]):
   641→    """Flag switch statements that have no default case."""
   642→    switch_re = re.compile(r"\bswitch\s*\([^)]*\)\s*\{")
   643→    for m in switch_re.finditer(content):
   644→        brace_start = m.end() - 1
   645→        depth = 0
   646→        body_end = None
   647→        for ci, ch, in_s in _scan_code(content, brace_start, min(brace_start + 5000, len(content))):
   648→            if in_s:
   649→                continue
   650→            if ch == "{":
   651→                depth += 1
   652→            elif ch == "}":
   653→                depth -= 1
   654→                if depth == 0:
   655→                    body_end = ci
   656→                    break
   657→
   658→        if body_end is None:
   659→            continue
   660→
   661→        body = content[brace_start + 1:body_end]
   662→        # Count case labels — only flag if there are actual cases
   663→        case_count = len(re.findall(r"\bcase\s+", body))
   664→        if case_count < 2:
   665→            continue
   666→
   667→        # Check for default: anywhere in the switch body
   668→        if re.search(r"\bdefault\s*:", body):
   669→            continue
   670→
   671→        line_no = content[:m.start()].count("\n") + 1
   672→        line_start = content.rfind("\n", 0, m.start()) + 1
   673→        line_end = content.find("\n", m.start())
   674→        if line_end == -1:
   675→            line_end = len(content)
   676→        smell_counts["switch_no_default"].append({
   677→            "file": filepath,
   678→            "line": line_no,
   679→            "content": content[line_start:line_end].strip()[:100],
   680→        })
   681→

   100→/**
   101→ * Load settings from generations.params.ui.editSettings
   102→ */
   103→async function loadGenerationSettings(generationId: string): Promise<GenerationEditSettings | null> {
   104→  const { data, error } = await supabase
   105→    .from('generations')
   106→    .select('params')
   107→    .eq('id', generationId)
   108→    .maybeSingle();
   109→
   110→  if (error) {
   111→    return null;
   112→  }
   113→
   114→  if (!data) {
   115→    return null;
   116→  }
   117→
   118→  const savedSettings = (data?.params as Record<string, unknown>)?.ui as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
   119→  const editSettings = savedSettings?.editSettings as Partial<GenerationEditSettings> | undefined;
   120→
   121→  if (editSettings) {
   122→    return {
   123→      ...DEFAULT_EDIT_SETTINGS,
   124→      ...editSettings,
   125→    };
   126→  }
   127→
   128→  return null;
   129→}
   130→
   131→/**
   132→ * Save settings to generations.params.ui.editSettings
   133→ */
   134→async function saveGenerationSettings(generationId: string, settings: GenerationEditSettings): Promise<void> {
   135→  // Fetch current params to merge
   136→  const { data: current, error: fetchError } = await supabase
   137→    .from('generations')
   138→    .select('params')
   139→    .eq('id', generationId)
   140→    .maybeSingle();
   141→
   142→  if (fetchError) {
   143→    throw new Error(`Failed to fetch current params: ${fetchError.message}`);
   144→  }
   145→
   146→  if (!current) {
   147→    // Generation was deleted, skip save
   148→    return;
   149→  }
   150→
   151→  // Merge with existing params
   152→  const currentParams = (current?.params || {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
   153→  const currentUi = (currentParams.ui || {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
   154→  const updatedParams = {
   155→    ...currentParams,
   156→    ui: {
   157→      ...currentUi,
   158→      editSettings: settings,
   159→      // Also save editMode at top level for backwards compatibility
   160→      editMode: settings.editMode,
   161→    }
   162→  };
   163→
   164→  const { error: updateError } = await supabase
   165→    .from('generations')
   166→    .update({ params: updatedParams })
   167→    .eq('id', generationId);
   168→
   169→  if (updateError) {
   170→    throw new Error(`Failed to save settings: ${updateError.message}`);
   171→  }
   172→}
   173→
   174→/**
   175→ * Hook for managing per-generation edit settings persistence
   176→ *
   177→ * Uses useAutoSaveSettings (customLoadSave mode) for the core persistence logic:
   178→ * - Status machine (idle → loading → ready → saving)
   179→ * - Debounced auto-save
   180→ * - Pending refs protection
   181→ * - Flush on unmount/entity change
   182→ *
   183→ * Saves to: generations.params.ui.editSettings
   184→ */
   185→export function useGenerationEditSettings({
   186→  generationId,
   187→  enabled = true,
   188→}: UseGenerationEditSettingsProps): UseGenerationEditSettingsReturn {
   189→  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
   190→
   191→  // Use the base persistent state hook (customLoadSave mode)

   201→    debounceMs: 500,
   202→    enabled,
   203→    debugTag: '[useGenerationEditSettings]',
   204→    customLoadSave: {
   205→      entityId: generationId,
   206→      load: loadGenerationSettings,
   207→      save: saveGenerationSettings,
   208→      onFlush: (entityId) => {
   209→        // Invalidate generation queries after flush
   210→        queryClient.invalidateQueries({
   211→          [REDACTED](entityId)
   212→        });
   213→      },
   214→    },
   215→    onSaveSuccess: () => {
   216→      if (generationId) {
   217→        queryClient.invalidateQueries({
   218→          [REDACTED](generationId)
   219→        });
   220→      }
   221→    },
   222→  });
   223→
   224→  // Individual setters that delegate to updateField
   225→  const setEditMode = useCallback((mode: EditMode) => {
   226→    updateField('editMode', mode);
   227→  }, [updateField]);
   228→
   229→  const setLoraMode = useCallback((mode: LoraMode) => {
   230→    updateField('loraMode', mode);
   231→  }, [updateField]);
   232→
   233→  const setCustomLoraUrl = useCallback((url: string) => {
   234→    updateField('customLoraUrl', url);
   235→  }, [updateField]);
   236→
   237→  const setNumGenerations = useCallback((num: number) => {
   238→    updateField('numGenerations', num);
   239→  }, [updateField]);
   240→
   241→  const setPrompt = useCallback((prompt: string) => {
   242→    updateField('prompt', prompt);
   243→  }, [updateField]);
   244→
   245→  const setQwenEditModel = useCallback((model: QwenEditModel) => {
   246→    updateField('qwenEditModel', model);
   247→  }, [updateField]);
   248→
   249→  const setImg2imgPrompt = useCallback((prompt: string) => {
   250→    updateFields({ img2imgPrompt: prompt, img2imgPromptHasBeenSet: true });
   251→  }, [updateFields]);
   252→
   253→  const setImg2imgStrength = useCallback((strength: number) => {
   254→    updateField('img2imgStrength', strength);
   255→  }, [updateField]);
   256→
   257→  const setImg2imgEnablePromptExpansion = useCallback((enabled: boolean) => {
   258→    updateField('img2imgEnablePromptExpansion', enabled);
   259→  }, [updateField]);
   260→
   261→  // Advanced settings setter (merges with existing)
   262→  const setAdvancedSettings = useCallback((updates: Partial<EditAdvancedSettings>) => {
   263→    updateFields({
   264→      advancedSettings: { ...settings.advancedSettings, ...updates },
   265→    });
   266→  }, [updateFields, settings.advancedSettings]);
   267→
   268→  // Video enhance settings setter (merges with existing)
   269→  const setEnhanceSettings = useCallback((updates: Partial<VideoEnhanceSettings>) => {
   270→    updateFields({

   272→    });
   273→  }, [updateFields, settings.enhanceSettings]);
   274→
   275→  const setCreateAsGeneration = useCallback((value: boolean) => {
   276→    updateField('createAsGeneration', value);
   277→  }, [updateField]);
   278→
   279→  // Bulk update
   280→  const updateSettings = useCallback((updates: Partial<GenerationEditSettings>) => {
   281→    updateFields(updates);
   282→  }, [updateFields]);
   283→
   284→  // Initialize from "last used" - wraps initializeFrom with prompt exclusion
   285→  const initializeFromLastUsed = useCallback((lastUsed: SyncedEditSettings & { editMode: EditMode }) => {
   286→    // Apply last used but never inherit prompts
   287→    initializeFrom({
   288→      ...lastUsed,
   289→      prompt: '',
   290→      img2imgPrompt: '',
   291→      img2imgPromptHasBeenSet: false,
   292→    });
   293→  }, [initializeFrom]);
   294→
   295→  return {
   296→    settings,
   297→    setEditMode,

   304→    setImg2imgStrength,
   305→    setImg2imgEnablePromptExpansion,
   306→    setAdvancedSettings,
   307→    setEnhanceSettings,
   308→    setCreateAsGeneration,
   309→    updateSettings,
   310→    isLoading: status === 'loading' || status === 'idle',
   311→    hasPersistedSettings,
   312→    initializeFromLastUsed,
   313→  };
   314→}

1→/**
     2→ * ImageEditCanvasContext
     3→ *
     4→ * Canvas/tool state for image editing in the lightbox.
     5→ * Contains mode, brush, annotation, canvas interaction, reposition,
     6→ * display refs, and panel position state.
     7→ *
     8→ * Consumers: LightboxLayout, FloatingToolControls, MediaDisplayWithCanvas, InfoPanel.
     9→ * Split from ImageEditContext to avoid re-renders from form/status changes.
    10→ */
    11→
    12→import React, { createContext, useContext } from 'react';
    13→import type { BrushStroke, AnnotationMode } from '../hooks/inpainting/types';
    14→import type { ImageTransform } from '../hooks/useRepositionMode';
    15→import type { StrokeOverlayHandle } from '../components/StrokeOverlay';
    16→
    17→// ============================================================================
    18→// Types
    19→// ============================================================================
    20→
    21→export type ImageEditMode = 'inpaint' | 'annotate' | 'reposition' | 'img2img' | 'text' | 'upscale' | null;
    22→
    23→export interface ImageEditCanvasState {
    24→  // Edit mode
    25→  isInpaintMode: boolean;
    26→  isMagicEditMode: boolean;
    27→  isSpecialEditMode: boolean;
    28→  editMode: ImageEditMode;
    29→  setIsInpaintMode: (value: boolean) => void;
    30→  setIsMagicEditMode: (value: boolean) => void;
    31→  setEditMode: (mode: ImageEditMode) => void;
    32→  handleEnterInpaintMode: () => void;
    33→  handleExitInpaintMode: () => void;
    34→  handleEnterMagicEditMode: () => void;
    35→  handleExitMagicEditMode: () => void;
    36→
    37→  // Brush tool
    38→  brushSize: number;
    39→  setBrushSize: (size: number) => void;
    40→  isEraseMode: boolean;
    41→  setIsEraseMode: (value: boolean) => void;
    42→  brushStrokes: BrushStroke[];
    43→  handleUndo: () => void;
    44→  handleClearMask: () => void;
    45→  inpaintPanelPosition: 'left' | 'right';
    46→  setInpaintPanelPosition: (pos: 'left' | 'right') => void;
    47→
    48→  // Annotation tool
    49→  isAnnotateMode: boolean;
    50→  setIsAnnotateMode: (value: boolean | ((prev: boolean) => boolean)) => void;
    51→  annotationMode: AnnotationMode;
    52→  setAnnotationMode: (mode: AnnotationMode | ((prev: AnnotationMode) => AnnotationMode)) => void;
    53→  selectedShapeId: string | null;
    54→
    55→  // Canvas interaction
    56→  onStrokeComplete: (stroke: BrushStroke) => void;
    57→  onStrokesChange: (strokes: BrushStroke[]) => void;
    58→  onSelectionChange: (shapeId: string | null) => void;
    59→  onTextModeHint: () => void;
    60→  strokeOverlayRef: React.RefObject<StrokeOverlayHandle>;
    61→  getDeleteButtonPosition: () => { x: number; y: number } | null;
    62→  handleToggleFreeForm: () => void;
    63→  handleDeleteSelected: () => void;
    64→
    65→  // Reposition
    66→  repositionTransform: ImageTransform | null;
    67→  hasTransformChanges: boolean;
    68→  isRepositionDragging: boolean;
    69→  repositionDragHandlers: {
    70→    onPointerDown: (e: React.PointerEvent) => void;
    71→    onPointerMove: (e: React.PointerEvent) => void;
    72→    onPointerUp: (e: React.PointerEvent) => void;
    73→    onPointerCancel: (e: React.PointerEvent) => void;
    74→    onWheel: (e: React.WheelEvent) => void;
    75→  } | null;
    76→  getTransformStyle: () => string;
    77→  setScale: (value: number) => void;
    78→  setRotation: (value: number) => void;
    79→  toggleFlipH: () => void;
    80→  toggleFlipV: () => void;
    81→  resetTransform: () => void;
    82→
    83→  // Display refs
    84→  imageContainerRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>;
    85→  isFlippedHorizontally: boolean;
    86→  isSaving: boolean;
    87→}
    88→
    89→// ============================================================================
    90→// Defaults
    91→// ============================================================================
    92→
    93→const EMPTY_CANVAS: ImageEditCanvasState = {
    94→  // Edit mode
    95→  isInpaintMode: false,
    96→  isMagicEditMode: false,
    97→  isSpecialEditMode: false,
    98→  editMode: null,
    99→  setIsInpaintMode: () => {},
   100→  setIsMagicEditMode: () => {},
   101→  setEditMode: () => {},
   102→  handleEnterInpaintMode: () => {},
   103→  handleExitInpaintMode: () => {},
   104→  handleEnterMagicEditMode: () => {},
   105→  handleExitMagicEditMode: () => {},
   106→
   107→  // Brush tool
   108→  brushSize: 20,
   109→  setBrushSize: () => {},
   110→  isEraseMode: false,
   111→  setIsEraseMode: () => {},
   112→  brushStrokes: [],
   113→  handleUndo: () => {},
   114→  handleClearMask: () => {},
   115→  inpaintPanelPosition: 'right',
   116→  setInpaintPanelPosition: () => {},
   117→
   118→  // Annotation tool
   119→  isAnnotateMode: false,
   120→  setIsAnnotateMode: () => {},
   121→  annotationMode: null,
   122→  setAnnotationMode: () => {},
   123→  selectedShapeId: null,
   124→
   125→  // Canvas interaction
   126→  onStrokeComplete: () => {},
   127→  onStrokesChange: () => {},
   128→  onSelectionChange: () => {},
   129→  onTextModeHint: () => {},
   130→  strokeOverlayRef: { current: null } as React.RefObject<StrokeOverlayHandle>,
   131→  getDeleteButtonPosition: () => null,
   132→  handleToggleFreeForm: () => {},
   133→  handleDeleteSelected: () => {},
   134→
   135→  // Reposition
   136→  repositionTransform: null,
   137→  hasTransformChanges: false,
   138→  isRepositionDragging: false,
   139→  repositionDragHandlers: null,
   140→  getTransformStyle: () => '',
   141→  setScale: () => {},
   142→  setRotation: () => {},
   143→  toggleFlipH: () => {},
   144→  toggleFlipV: () => {},
   145→  resetTransform: () => {},
   146→
   147→  // Display refs
   148→  imageContainerRef: { current: null } as React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>,
   149→  isFlippedHorizontally: false,
   150→  isSaving: false,
   151→};
   152→
   153→// ============================================================================
   154→// Context + Hook
   155→// ============================================================================
   156→
   157→const ImageEditCanvasContext = createContext<ImageEditCanvasState | null>(null);
   158→
   159→interface ImageEditCanvasProviderProps {
   160→  children: React.ReactNode;
   161→  value: ImageEditCanvasState;
   162→}
   163→
   164→export const ImageEditCanvasProvider: React.FC<ImageEditCanvasProviderProps> = ({
   165→  children,
   166→  value,
   167→}) => {
   168→  return (
   169→    <ImageEditCanvasContext.Provider value={value}>
   170→      {children}
   171→    </ImageEditCanvasContext.Provider>
   172→  );
   173→};
   174→
   175→/**
   176→ * Returns canvas/tool edit state, or safe defaults when used outside provider.
   177→ */
   178→export function useImageEditCanvasSafe(): ImageEditCanvasState {
   179→  const context = useContext(ImageEditCanvasContext);
   180→  return context ?? EMPTY_CANVAS;
   181→}
   182→

   125→export const MotionControl: React.FC<MotionControlProps> = ({
   126→  motionMode,

   159→  const isCustomConfig = !selectedPhasePresetId;

   161→  // Get the built-in default preset for the current mode (I2V vs VACE)

   163→  const builtinDefaultPreset = useMemo(() => {

   166→
   167→  const builtinDefaultId = useMemo(() => {
   168→    return generationTypeMode === 'vace' ? BUILTIN_DEFAULT_VACE_ID : BUILTIN_DEFAULT_I2V_ID;
   169→  }, [generationTypeMode]);
   170→
   171→  // Fetch additional featured presets from database (optional)
   172→  const { data: additionalPresets } = useQuery({
   173→    [REDACTED](featuredPresetIds),
   174→    queryFn: async () => {
   175→      if (!featuredPresetIds || featuredPresetIds.length === 0) return [];
   176→      
   177→      const { data, error } = await supabase
   178→        .from('resources')
   179→        .select('*')
   180→        .in('id', featuredPresetIds);
   181→      
   182→      if (error) {
   183→        console.error('[MotionControl] Error fetching featured presets:', error);
   184→        return [];
   185→      }
   186→      
   187→      // Sort by the order in featuredPresetIds
   188→      const sorted = featuredPresetIds
   189→        .map(id => data?.find(p => p.id === id))
   190→        .filter(Boolean);
   191→      
   192→      return sorted;
   193→    },
   194→    enabled: featuredPresetIds.length > 0,
   195→    staleTime: 60000, // Cache for 1 minute
   196→    placeholderData: (prev) => prev, // Keep previous data during refetches to prevent flicker
   197→  });
   198→
   199→  // Combine built-in default (first) + additional presets from database
   200→  const allPresets = useMemo(() => {
   201→    const presets: Array<{ id: string; metadata: { name: string; description: string; phaseConfig: PhaseConfig; sample_generations?: PresetSampleGeneration[] } }> = [builtinDefaultPreset];
   202→    if (additionalPresets && additionalPresets.length > 0) {
   203→      presets.push(...additionalPresets);
   204→    }
   205→    return presets;
   206→  }, [builtinDefaultPreset, additionalPresets]);
   207→
   208→  // All known preset IDs (for determining if we show chips or SelectedPresetCard)
   209→  // Include BOTH builtin IDs to handle race conditions when hasStructureVideo changes
   210→  const allKnownPresetIds = useMemo(() => {

   268→  return (

   285→            {/* Show preset chips OR selected non-known preset card */}
   286→            {isSelectedPresetKnown ? (

   292→                    <Label className="text-sm font-medium">Motion Preset:</Label>

1→/**
     2→ * useGenerationInvalidation.ts
     3→ * 
     4→ * Centralized hook for invalidating generation-related React Query caches.
     5→ * 
     6→ * Why this exists:
     7→ * - 19 different places were calling invalidateQueries with different combinations
     8→ * - Inconsistent patterns led to stale data or excessive invalidation
     9→ * - No visibility into what triggered invalidations
    10→ * 
    11→ * Usage:
    12→ *   const invalidateGenerations = useInvalidateGenerations();
    13→ *   invalidateGenerations(shotId, { reason: 'delete-image' });
    14→ * 
    15→ * For variant changes (no shotId needed):
    16→ *   invalidateVariantChange(queryClient, { generationId, reason: 'set-primary' });
    17→ * 
    18→ * Scopes:
    19→ * - 'all': All generation-related queries (default)
    20→ * - 'images': Just image data (all-shot-generations, segment-live-timeline)
    21→ * - 'metadata': Just metadata (shot-generations-meta)
    22→ * - 'counts': Just counts (unpositioned-count)
    23→ *
    24→ * Note: Uses queryKeys registry from @/shared/lib/queryKeys for all key references.
    25→ */
    26→
    27→import { useQueryClient, QueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
    28→import { useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
    29→import { queryKeys } from '../../lib/queryKeys';
    30→
    31→export type InvalidationScope =
    32→  | 'all'           // All generation-related queries for a shot
    33→  | 'images'        // Just image data (all-shot-generations)
    34→  | 'metadata'      // Just metadata (shot-generations-meta)
    35→  | 'counts';       // Just counts (unpositioned-count)
    36→
    37→export interface InvalidationOptions {
    38→  /** Which queries to invalidate. Default: 'all' */
    39→  scope?: InvalidationScope;
    40→  /** Debug reason for logging. Required for traceability. */
    41→  reason: string;
    42→  /** Delay invalidation by this many ms (for batching rapid changes) */
    43→  delayMs?: number;
    44→  /** Also invalidate the 'shots' query for the project (e.g., for thumbnail updates) */
    45→  includeShots?: boolean;
    46→  /** Project ID - required if includeShots is true */
    47→  projectId?: string;
    48→  /** Also invalidate unified-generations at project level */
    49→  includeProjectUnified?: boolean;
    50→}
    51→
    52→/**
    53→ * Options for variant-specific invalidation.
    54→ * Use when a variant is created/updated/set-as-primary.
    55→ */
    56→export interface VariantInvalidationOptions {
    57→  /** Debug reason for logging. Required for traceability. */
    58→  reason: string;
    59→  /** Generation ID that the variant belongs to */
    60→  generationId: string;
    61→  /** Optional: specific shot ID to prioritize (will refetch immediately before global invalidation) */
    62→  shotId?: string;
    63→  /** Optional: project ID for project-scoped unified-generations invalidation */
    64→  projectId?: string;
    65→  /** Delay before invalidation (e.g., 100ms for DB trigger to complete) */
    66→  delayMs?: number;
    67→}
    68→
    69→/**
    70→ * Internal helper that performs the actual invalidation.
    71→ * Extracted so it can be used with or without React hooks.
    72→ */
    73→function performInvalidation(
    74→  queryClient: QueryClient,
    75→  shotId: string,
    76→  options: InvalidationOptions
    77→): void {
    78→  const { 
    79→    scope = 'all', 
    80→    includeShots = false,
    81→    projectId,
    82→    includeProjectUnified = false
    83→  } = options;
    84→  
    85→  // Debug logging via centralized config
    86→  
    87→  // Invalidate based on scope
    88→  if (scope === 'all' || scope === 'images') {
    89→    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED](shotId) });
    90→    // NOTE: ['shot-generations'] removed in Phase 3.5 - it was never queried, only invalidated
    91→    // Also invalidate segment-live-timeline so segment video positions update
    92→    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED](shotId) });
    93→  }
    94→
    95→  if (scope === 'all' || scope === 'metadata') {
    96→    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED](shotId) });
    97→  }
    98→
    99→  if (scope === 'all' || scope === 'counts') {
   100→    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED](shotId) });
   101→  }
   102→
   103→  // Optional: include shots query
   104→  if (includeShots && projectId) {
   105→    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED](projectId) });
   106→  }
   107→
   108→  // Optional: include project-level unified generations
   109→  if (includeProjectUnified && projectId) {
   110→    // Invalidate project-scoped unified generations (partial key match)
   111→    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED](projectId) });
   112→  }
   113→}
   114→
   115→/**
   116→ * Hook that returns a stable invalidation function.
   117→ * Use this in React components/hooks.
   118→ */
   119→export function useInvalidateGenerations() {
   120→  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
   121→  const timeoutRefs = useRef<Map<string, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>>(new Map());
   122→  
   123→  return useCallback((shotId: string, options: InvalidationOptions) => {
   124→    const { delayMs } = options;
   125→    
   126→    // If there's already a pending invalidation for this shotId, clear it
   127→    const existingTimeout = timeoutRefs.current.get(shotId);
   128→    if (existingTimeout) {
   129→      clearTimeout(existingTimeout);
   130→      timeoutRefs.current.delete(shotId);
   131→    }
   132→    
   133→    if (delayMs && delayMs > 0) {
   134→      // Delayed invalidation (useful for batching rapid changes)
   135→      const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
   136→        performInvalidation(queryClient, shotId, options);
   137→        timeoutRefs.current.delete(shotId);
   138→      }, delayMs);
   139→      timeoutRefs.current.set(shotId, timeout);
   140→    } else {
   141→      // Immediate invalidation
   142→      performInvalidation(queryClient, shotId, options);
   143→    }
   144→  }, [queryClient]);
   145→}
   146→
   147→/**
   148→ * Non-hook version for use outside React components (e.g., in event handlers).
   149→ * Requires passing in the queryClient.
   150→ */
   151→export function invalidateGenerationsSync(
   152→  queryClient: QueryClient,
   153→  shotId: string,
   154→  options: InvalidationOptions
   155→): void {
   156→  if (options.delayMs && options.delayMs > 0) {
   157→    setTimeout(() => {
   158→      performInvalidation(queryClient, shotId, options);
   159→    }, options.delayMs);
   160→  } else {
   161→    performInvalidation(queryClient, shotId, options);
   162→  }
   163→}
   164→
   165→/**
   166→ * Invalidate ALL shots (dangerous - use sparingly).
   167→ * This is for global events where we don't know which specific shot was affected.
   168→ * Logs a warning to encourage scoped invalidation.
   169→ */
   170→export function invalidateAllShotGenerations(
   171→  queryClient: QueryClient
   172→): void {
   173→
   174→  // Use predicate to invalidate all shot-generations queries regardless of shotId
   175→  queryClient.invalidateQueries({
   176→    predicate: (query) => query.queryKey[0] === queryKeys.generations.byShotAll[0]
   177→  });
   178→  // NOTE: ['shot-generations'] removed - it was never queried, only invalidated
   179→}
   180→
   181→/**
   182→ * Invalidate caches after a variant change (create, update, set-as-primary).
   183→ * 
   184→ * This handles the variant → generation → shot cascade:
   185→ * - A variant belongs to a generation
   186→ * - A generation can appear in multiple shots (via shot_generations)
   187→ * - When the primary variant changes, generations.location changes
   188→ * - This affects Timeline/Batch mode displays
   189→ * 
   190→ * @param queryClient - React Query client
   191→ * @param options - Invalidation options including generationId and reason
   192→ */
   193→export async function invalidateVariantChange(
   194→  queryClient: QueryClient,
   195→  options: VariantInvalidationOptions
   196→): Promise<void> {
   197→  const { generationId, shotId, projectId, delayMs } = options;
   198→  
   199→  // Apply delay if specified (e.g., for DB trigger to complete)
   200→  if (delayMs && delayMs > 0) {
   201→    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, delayMs));
   202→  }
   203→  
   204→  // 1. Invalidate variant-specific queries
   205→  queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED](generationId) });
   206→  queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED](generationId) });
   207→  // Also invalidate variant-badges so the "X new" badges update
   208→  queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED] });
   209→
   210→  // 2. If we know the shot, refetch it immediately for faster UI update
   211→  if (shotId) {
   212→    await queryClient.refetchQueries({ [REDACTED](shotId) });
   213→  }
   214→
   215→  // 3. Invalidate all shot-generations (generation might be in multiple shots)
   216→  queryClient.invalidateQueries({
   217→    predicate: (query) => query.queryKey[0] === queryKeys.generations.byShotAll[0]
   218→  });
   219→  // NOTE: ['shot-generations'] removed - it was never queried, only invalidated
   220→
   221→  // 4. Invalidate generation galleries - cover ALL key patterns for compatibility
   222→  queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED] });
   223→  queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED] });
   224→  // Derived items (variants/generations "based on this")
   225→  queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED] });
   226→
   227→  // 5. If projectId provided, also invalidate project-scoped queries
   228→  if (projectId) {
   229→    // Partial key match for project-scoped unified generations
   230→    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ [REDACTED](projectId) });
   231→  }
   232→
   233→  // 6. Invalidate segment output queries (for timeline segment strip)
   234→  // This ensures variant changes are reflected in the segment strip
   235→  queryClient.invalidateQueries({
   236→    predicate: (query) => query.queryKey[0] === queryKeys.segments.childrenAll[0]
   237→  });
   238→
   239→  // 7. Invalidate source image change detection (for video warning indicators)
   240→  // This ensures warnings appear immediately when source images change
   241→  queryClient.invalidateQueries({
   242→    predicate: (query) => query.queryKey[0] === queryKeys.segments.sourceSlotAll[0]
   243→  });
   244→}
   245→
   246→

1→/**
     2→ * Centralized error handling utility
     3→ *
     4→ * Provides consistent error handling across the app:
     5→ * - Categorizes errors by type
     6→ * - Logs with structured context
     7→ * - Shows appropriate user feedback via toast
     8→ * - Respects error type-specific behavior (silent errors, auth redirects, etc.)
     9→ */
    10→
    11→import { toast } from '@/shared/hooks/use-toast';
    12→import {
    13→  AppError,
    14→  categorizeError,
    15→  isNetworkError,
    16→  SilentError
    17→} from './errors';
    18→
    19→interface HandleErrorOptions {
    20→  /** Context string for logging (e.g., "ImageUpload", "TaskCreation") */
    21→  context: string;
    22→  /** Additional data to include in logs */
    23→  logData?: Record<string, unknown>;
    24→  /** Custom toast title (defaults based on error type) */
    25→  toastTitle?: string;
    26→  /** Override whether to show toast (null = use error's default) */
    27→  showToast?: boolean;
    28→  /** Callback after error is handled */
    29→  onError?: (error: AppError) => void;
    30→}
    31→
    32→/**
    33→ * Toast titles by error type
    34→ */
    35→const DEFAULT_TITLES: Record<string, string> = {
    36→  NetworkError: 'Connection Error',
    37→  AuthError: 'Authentication Required',
    38→  ValidationError: 'Invalid Input',
    39→  ServerError: 'Server Error',
    40→  AppError: 'Error',
    41→};
    42→
    43→/**
    44→ * Handle an error with consistent logging and user feedback
    45→ *
    46→ * @example
    47→ * ```typescript
    48→ * try {
    49→ *   await uploadImage(file);
    50→ * } catch (error) {
    51→ *   handleError(error, { context: 'ImageUpload', logData: { fileName: file.name } });
    52→ * }
    53→ * ```
    54→ */
    55→export function handleError(error: unknown, options: HandleErrorOptions): AppError {
    56→  const { context, logData, toastTitle, showToast, onError } = options;
    57→
    58→  // Categorize the error
    59→  const appError = categorizeError(error, { context, ...logData });
    60→
    61→  // Build log tag
    62→  const tag = `[${context}]`;
    63→
    64→  // Log the error with context
    65→  if (appError instanceof SilentError) {
    66→  } else {
    67→    // Regular errors get error-level logging
    68→    console.error(`${tag} ${appError.name}:`, appError.message, {
    69→      errorType: appError.name,
    70→      cause: appError.cause?.message,
    71→      ...appError.context,
    72→    });
    73→  }
    74→
    75→  // Determine if we should show a toast
    76→  const shouldShowToast = showToast ?? appError.showToast;
    77→
    78→  if (shouldShowToast) {
    79→    const title = toastTitle ?? DEFAULT_TITLES[appError.name] ?? DEFAULT_TITLES.AppError;
    80→
    81→    // Use the error's message or fall back to a generic message
    82→    let description = appError.message;
    83→
    84→    // For network errors, provide more helpful messages
    85→    if (isNetworkError(appError)) {
    86→      if (appError.isOffline) {
    87→        description = 'You appear to be offline. Please check your connection.';
    88→      } else if (appError.isTimeout) {
    89→        description = 'Request timed out. Please try again.';
    90→      }
    91→    }
    92→
    93→    toast({
    94→      title,
    95→      description,
    96→      variant: 'destructive',
    97→    });
    98→  }
    99→
   100→  // Call optional callback
   101→  onError?.(appError);
   102→
   103→  return appError;
   104→}
   105→
   106→
   107→// Re-export error types for convenience
   108→export {
   109→  AppError,
   110→  NetworkError,
   111→  AuthError,
   112→  SilentError,
   113→  isAppError,
   114→  isAuthError,
   115→  isNetworkError,
   116→} from './errors';
   117→
